" Report starts here

* Change has been reverted, transaction is not operational with limits                      &nb
* content separation changes:                                   &
* sm50 is not allowed to be used in non-system-clients                          
*CALL METHOD cl_cos_utilities=>DISABLE_WITH_MSG_AND_LEAVE                               
*  EXPORTING                                       
*    io_client    = cl_cos_client=>sc_application_client                         &
*    iv_component = 'SM50'.                                 

" Inform about reduced function set for non system clients
PERFORM START.

AT SELECTION-SCREEN OUTPUT.
  " If the user no authorization to display the ABAP call stack,
  " disable the checkbox in the SM50 configuration screen.
  IF ( IN_SM50_CONFIG_SCREEN = ABAP_TRUE ) AND ( ABAP_STACK_AUTH <> ABAP_TRUE ).
    IN_SM50_CONFIG_SCREEN = ABAP_FALSE.
    LOOP AT SCREEN.
      IF SCREEN-NAME = 'WITHRINF'.
        SCREEN-INPUT = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    MESSAGE S674.
  ENDIF.

FORM SET_ACT_WPS_TEXT USING WRUN TYPE ABAP_BOOL.
  DATA: TEXT(64).

  IF WRUN = 'X'.
    TEXT = '@UQ@All Work Processes'(093) ##TEXT_DIFF.
  ELSE.
    TEXT = '@UQ@Active Work Processes Only'(091) ##TEXT_DIFF.
  ENDIF.

  " Seems that icons are not displayed correctly. Therefore deactivated this.
  "CALL FUNCTION 'ICON_CREATE'
  "  exporting name = 'ICON_ACTIVITY'
  "            text = text
  "            "info = text
  "  importing result = act_wps_text.
  ACT_WPS_TEXT = TEXT.
ENDFORM.


FORM START.

  " Read and check permissions
  IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT = CL_SYSTEM_CLIENT_CHECK=>IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT( ).

  " Check whether the user has authorization to display the ABAP call stack
  AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'S_ADMI_FCD'
    ID 'S_ADMI_FCD' FIELD 'PADM'.
  IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
    ABAP_STACK_AUTH = ABAP_FALSE.
  ELSE.
    ABAP_STACK_AUTH = ABAP_TRUE.
  ENDIF.

  " Not needed for CGAP
  "if is_system_client = 'X'.
  "  restricted_view = '0'.
  "else.
  "  restricted_view = '1'.
  "endif.

  IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X'.
    MESSAGE S168.
  ENDIF.

  DATA: L_VALUE TYPE STRING.

************************************************************************                              &nb
* Initialize TRC structur                                    
************************************************************************                              &nb
  CLEAR TRC.
  TRC-MAIN  = 'X'.
  TRC-LEVEL = '1'.
  TRC-ID    = 0.

  " Special treatment for field sys_list:
  " When started via TCODE SM66 sys_list = 'X' and must not be overwritten
  " or reset.

  GET PARAMETER ID 'SM50_CONFIGURATION' FIELD SM50_CONFIG.
  IF SY-SUBRC = 4.
    WITHHEAD = 'X'.
    LOADDISP = ' '.
    WRUN = ' '.
    WITHRINF = 'X'.
    WITHDD = ' '.
    SM50_CONFIG(1) = 'X'.    " with head
    SM50_CONFIG+1(1) = ' '.  " load as percent
    SM50_CONFIG+2(1) = ' '.  " only active work processes
    SM50_CONFIG+3(1) = 'X'.  " with abap stack
    SM50_CONFIG+4(1) = ' '.  " classic detail info display
    SM50_CONFIG+5(1) = ' '.  " systemwide list
    SM50_CONFIG+6(1) = ' '.  " show sql
    SM50_CONFIG+7(1) = ' '.  " show communication
    SM50_CONFIG+8(1) = ' '.  " ignore serverstate
    SM50_CONFIG+9(1) = ' '.  " no extended info area
    SM50_CONFIG+10(1) = 'X'. " simple view of wp trace files

    SET PARAMETER ID 'SM50_CONFIGURATION' FIELD SM50_CONFIG.
  ELSE.
    WITHHEAD = SM50_CONFIG(1).
    LOADDISP = SM50_CONFIG+1(1).
    WRUN     = SM50_CONFIG+2(1).
    WITHRINF = SM50_CONFIG+3(1).
    WITHDD   = SM50_CONFIG+4(1).
    IF SYS_LIST = ' '.
      SYS_LIST = SM50_CONFIG+5(1).
    ENDIF.
    SQLDISP  = SM50_CONFIG+6(1).
    COMDISP  = SM50_CONFIG+7(1).
    IGNSTATE = SM50_CONFIG+8(1).
    EXTINFO =  SM50_CONFIG+9(1).
    SIMPTRC =  SM50_CONFIG+10(1).
  ENDIF.

  " If the user has no authorization to display the ABAP call stack,
  " disable correspondig checkbox "WITHRINF".
  IF ABAP_STACK_AUTH = ABAP_FALSE.
    WITHRINF = ' '.
  ENDIF.

  SYS_WRUN = WRUN.
  SYS_L2 = SYS_LIST.
  ORIG_WITHHEAD = WITHHEAD.
  ORIG_EXTINFO = EXTINFO.
  ORIG_LOADDISP = LOADDISP.
  ORIG_WITHRINF = WITHRINF.
  ORIG_WITHDD = WITHDD.
  ORIG_SYS_LIST = SYS_LIST.
  ORIG_SQLDISP = SQLDISP.
  ORIG_COMDISP = COMDISP.
  ORIG_IGNSTATE = IGNSTATE.
  ORIG_SIMPTRC = SIMPTRC.

  PERFORM SET_ACT_WPS_TEXT USING WRUN.

  "get my server name
  MYNAME = CL_ABAP_SYST=>GET_INSTANCE_NAME( ).

  CL_SPFL_PROFILE_PARAMETER=>GET_VALUE(
    EXPORTING
      NAME  = 'SAPSYSTEMNAME'
    IMPORTING
      VALUE = L_VALUE ).
  MYSYSTEM = L_VALUE.

************************************************************************                              &nb
* set up alv grid                                    &n
************************************************************************                              &nb

  IF SYS_LIST = 'X'.
    PERFORM SHOW_SYSTEM_WIDE_LIST.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM BUILD_LOCAL_LIST.
  PERFORM BUILD_FIELDCAT USING FIELDCAT[] '1'.
  PERFORM BUILD_LAYOUT USING LAYOUT.

  PERFORM BUILD_EXTAB USING EXTAB[].

  IS_VARIANT-HANDLE = 'LIST'.

  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY'
    EXPORTING
      I_STRUCTURE_NAME         = 'WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV'
      I_CALLBACK_PROGRAM       = SY-REPID
      I_CALLBACK_PF_STATUS_SET = 'SM50_SET_STATUS'
      I_CALLBACK_USER_COMMAND  = 'SM50_USER_CMD'
      I_SAVE                   = 'A'
      IT_FIELDCAT              = FIELDCAT[]
      IS_LAYOUT                = LAYOUT
      IT_EXCLUDING             = EXTAB[]
      IS_VARIANT               = IS_VARIANT
      IT_EVENTS                = EVENTS[]
    TABLES
      T_OUTTAB                 = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.

ENDFORM.

FORM SHOW_SYSTEM_WIDE_LIST.
  DATA: LS_EXIT TYPE SLIS_EXIT_BY_USER.

  PERFORM BUILD_SYSTEM_LIST.
  PERFORM BUILD_FIELDCAT USING SYSTEM_FIELDCAT[] '0'.
  PERFORM BUILD_SYSTEM_LAYOUT USING SYSTEM_LAYOUT.

  IS_VARIANT-HANDLE = 'SYS'.
  SYSTEM_WIDE_LIST = 'X'.

* Start of ALV Migration changes - C5056145                             &nb
*call function 'REUSE_ALV_LIST_DISPLAY'                                  &n
* End of ALV Migration changes - C5056145                              
  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY'
    EXPORTING
      I_STRUCTURE_NAME         = 'WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV'
      I_CALLBACK_PROGRAM       = SY-REPID
      I_CALLBACK_PF_STATUS_SET = 'SM50_SYSTEM_SET_STATUS'
      I_CALLBACK_USER_COMMAND  = 'SM50_SYSTEM_USER_CMD'
      I_SAVE                   = 'A'
      IT_FIELDCAT              = SYSTEM_FIELDCAT[]
      "it_events                = sw_session_events[]
      IT_EVENTS                = EVENTS[]
      IS_LAYOUT                = SYSTEM_LAYOUT
      IS_VARIANT               = IS_VARIANT
    IMPORTING
      ES_EXIT_CAUSED_BY_USER   = LS_EXIT
    TABLES
      T_OUTTAB                 = SYSTEM_WORKER_LIST_ALV.

  CLEAR SYSTEM_WIDE_LIST.
ENDFORM.

" There might be persistent fieldcat enitries in table LTDX from previous
" of RSMON000_ALV. These are no longer valid because the field names changed.
" This form will remove all old entries for this user.
FORM RESET_FIELDCAT_IN_DB.

  DELETE FROM LTDX WHERE REPORT = SY-REPID AND USERNAME = SY-UNAME. "#EC CI_NOFIRST
  COMMIT WORK.
ENDFORM.

FORM SET_SM50_CONFIGURATION USING LOCAL_LIST TYPE I.
  CLEAR SM50_CONFIG.

  IF LOCAL_LIST = 0.
    WRUN = SYS_WRUN.
    SYS_LIST = SYS_L2.
  ENDIF.

  IF WITHHEAD = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF LOADDISP = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+1(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF WRUN = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+2(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF WITHRINF = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+3(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF WITHDD = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+4(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF SYS_LIST = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+5(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF SQLDISP = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+6(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF COMDISP = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+7(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF IGNSTATE = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+8(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF EXTINFO = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+9(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.
  IF SIMPTRC = 'X'.
    SM50_CONFIG+10(1) = 'X'.
  ENDIF.

  SYS_WRUN = WRUN.
  SYS_L2 = SYS_LIST.

  PERFORM SET_ACT_WPS_TEXT USING WRUN.

  SET PARAMETER ID 'SM50_CONFIGURATION' FIELD SM50_CONFIG.
  UPDATE USR05 SET PARVA = SM50_CONFIG
    WHERE BNAME = SY-UNAME AND
          PARID = 'SM50_CONFIGURATION'.
  IF SY-SUBRC = 4.
    DATA: USR05ENTRY TYPE USR05.
    USR05ENTRY-MANDT = SY-MANDT.
    USR05ENTRY-BNAME = SY-UNAME.
    USR05ENTRY-PARID = 'SM50_CONFIGURATION'.
    USR05ENTRY-PARVA = SM50_CONFIG.
    INSERT INTO USR05 VALUES USR05ENTRY.
  ENDIF.
  COMMIT WORK.
ENDFORM.

FORM COPY_DISP_TO_ALV USING DISP_TAB TYPE SSI_WORKER_LIST_DISP
                      CHANGING ALV_TAB TYPE WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV_TAB.
  DATA: WORKER_INFO_DISP     TYPE SSI_WORKER_INFO_DISP,
        WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV TYPE WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV,
        TMP_FIELD_COL        TYPE SLIS_SPECIALCOL_ALV,
        ELAPSED_TIME_LIMIT   TYPE I,
        MARK_LINE            TYPE I.

  LOOP AT DISP_TAB INTO WORKER_INFO_DISP.
    MARK_LINE = 0.

    CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING WORKER_INFO_DISP TO WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV.

    " Add some colors

    " - if developer trace is activated
    IF WORKER_INFO_DISP-TRACE_LEVEL > 1.
      MARK_LINE = 1.
    ENDIF.

    " - if workprocess crashed
    IF WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-FAILURES > 0.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-FIELDNAME = 'FAILURES'.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-COL = COL_NEGATIVE.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-INT = 0.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-INV = 0.
      APPEND TMP_FIELD_COL TO WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-FIELD_COL.
    ENDIF.

    " - if current elapsed time exceeds a limit
    CASE WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-PRIORITY.
      WHEN RQ_Q_PRIO_HIGH.   ELAPSED_TIME_LIMIT = 60.
      WHEN RQ_Q_PRIO_NORMAL. ELAPSED_TIME_LIMIT = 600.
      WHEN RQ_Q_PRIO_LOW.    ELAPSED_TIME_LIMIT = -1.
    ENDCASE.

    IF ELAPSED_TIME_LIMIT > 0 AND
       WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-ELAPSED_TIME > ELAPSED_TIME_LIMIT.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-FIELDNAME = 'ELAPSED_TIME'.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-COL = COL_TOTAL.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-INT = 0.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-INV = 0.
      APPEND TMP_FIELD_COL TO WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-FIELD_COL.
    ENDIF.

    " - if worker  holds a lock
    IF WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-SEM_LOCKED IS NOT INITIAL.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-FIELDNAME = 'SEM_LOCKED'.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-COL = COL_NEGATIVE.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-INT = 0.
      TMP_FIELD_COL-COLOR-INV = 0.
      APPEND TMP_FIELD_COL TO WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-FIELD_COL.
    ENDIF.

    IF WORKER_INFO_DISP-RECONNECT_STATE IS NOT INITIAL.
      MARK_LINE = 1.
    ENDIF.

    IF MARK_LINE =  1.
      WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-LINE_COL = 'C30'.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV-LINE_COL.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV TO ALV_TAB.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.

FORM BUILD_LOCAL_LIST.
  DATA: SERVER_NAME TYPE SSI_SERVERNAME.

  CLEAR WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV[].
  SERVER_NAME = SPACE.
  PERFORM BUILD_LIST_OF_SERVER USING SERVER_NAME
                               CHANGING WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV[].
ENDFORM.

FORM BUILD_SYSTEM_LIST.
  DATA: SERVER_LIST      TYPE SSI_SERVER_LIST,
        SERVER_INFO_LINE TYPE SSI_SERVER_INFO,
        MSG(80).

  CLEAR SYSTEM_WORKER_LIST_ALV.

  " Get process list of all servers and collect them in system_worker_list_alv.
  SERVER_LIST = CL_SYSTEM_INFO=>GET_SERVER_LIST( ONLY_ACTIVE_SERVER = 0 ).
  LOOP AT SERVER_LIST INTO SERVER_INFO_LINE.

    IF SERVER_INFO_LINE-SERVER_NAME <> MYNAME.
      " skip server in strange states
      IF SERVER_INFO_LINE-STATE = IF_SERVER_STATES=>STOP.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
      IF IGNSTATE = ' ' AND
         SERVER_INFO_LINE-STATE <> IF_SERVER_STATES=>RUNNING.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF COMDISP = 'X'.
      MSG = TEXT-490.
      REPLACE '&' IN MSG WITH SERVER_INFO_LINE-SERVER_NAME.
      CALL FUNCTION 'SAPGUI_PROGRESS_INDICATOR'
        EXPORTING
          TEXT   = MSG
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS = 0.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM BUILD_LIST_OF_SERVER USING SERVER_INFO_LINE-SERVER_NAME
                                 CHANGING SYSTEM_WORKER_LIST_ALV.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.

FORM BUILD_LIST_OF_SERVER USING SERVER_NAME TYPE SSI_SERVERNAME
                          CHANGING LIST_ALV TYPE WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV_TAB.
  DATA: SERVER_INFO        TYPE REF TO CL_SERVER_INFO,
        WORKER_LIST        TYPE SSI_WORKER_LIST,
        ONLY_ACTIVE_WORKER TYPE SSI_BOOL.

  CLEAR WORKER_LIST.
  CLEAR WORKER_LIST_DISP.

  TRY.
      CREATE OBJECT SERVER_INFO
        EXPORTING
          SERVER_NAME = SERVER_NAME.

      IF WRUN = 'X'.
        ONLY_ACTIVE_WORKER = 1.
      ELSE.
        ONLY_ACTIVE_WORKER = 0.
      ENDIF.

      WORKER_LIST = SERVER_INFO->GET_WORKER_LIST( WITH_APPLICATION_INFO = 1
                                                  WITH_CPU              = 1 "with_cpu
                                                  ONLY_ACTIVE_WORKER    = ONLY_ACTIVE_WORKER ).
    CATCH CX_SSI_NO_AUTH.
      MESSAGE E150 WITH TEXT-540.
      "MESSAGE ID 14 TYPE 'E' NUMBER 150 WITH text-540.
  ENDTRY.

  WORKER_LIST_DISP =
    WORKER_LIST_HANDLER=>CONV_LIST_FOR_DISPLAY(
    EXPORTING
      WORKER_LIST     = WORKER_LIST
      RESTRICTED_VIEW = RESTRICTED_VIEW ).

  PERFORM COPY_DISP_TO_ALV USING WORKER_LIST_DISP CHANGING LIST_ALV.
  PERFORM BUILD_EVENT USING EVENTS[].

ENDFORM.

* build list events                                     
FORM BUILD_EVENT USING C_EVENT TYPE SLIS_T_EVENT.
  DATA: LS_EVENT TYPE SLIS_ALV_EVENT.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <LS_EVENT> TYPE SLIS_ALV_EVENT.

* TODO: find out how to refresh the events of the current alv.                      &
*       if this can be done, no restart is needed.                     &nbs
  IF ( ORIG_WITHHEAD <> WITHHEAD ) OR
     ( ORIG_WITHRINF <> WITHRINF ) OR
     ( ORIG_WITHDD <> WITHDD ) OR
     ( ORIG_LOADDISP <> LOADDISP ) OR
     ( ORIG_SYS_LIST <> SYS_LIST ) OR
     ( ORIG_SQLDISP <> SQLDISP ) OR
     ( ORIG_COMDISP <> COMDISP ) OR
     ( ORIG_IGNSTATE <> IGNSTATE ) OR
     ( ORIG_EXTINFO <> EXTINFO ) OR
     ( ORIG_SIMPTRC <> SIMPTRC ).
    SM50_CONFIG(1) = WITHHEAD.
    SM50_CONFIG+1(1) = LOADDISP.
    SM50_CONFIG+2(1) = WRUN.
    SM50_CONFIG+3(1) = WITHRINF.
    SM50_CONFIG+4(1) = WITHDD.
    SM50_CONFIG+6(1) = SQLDISP.
    SM50_CONFIG+7(1) = COMDISP.
    SM50_CONFIG+8(1) = IGNSTATE.
    SM50_CONFIG+9(1) = EXTINFO.
    SM50_CONFIG+10(1) = SIMPTRC.
    SET PARAMETER ID 'SM50_CONFIGURATION' FIELD SM50_CONFIG.
    PERFORM RESTART_SM50.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT C_EVENT ASSIGNING <LS_EVENT>.
    IF <LS_EVENT>-NAME = 'TOP_OF_PAGE' AND
       <LS_EVENT>-FORM = 'CREATE_HEADER'.
      DELETE C_EVENT INDEX SY-TABIX.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR LS_EVENT.

  IF WITHHEAD = 'X'.
    LS_EVENT-NAME = 'TOP_OF_PAGE'.
    LS_EVENT-FORM = 'CREATE_HEADER'.
    APPEND LS_EVENT TO C_EVENT.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "BUILD_EVENT

FORM RESTART_SM50.
  SUBMIT RSMON000_ALV_NEW USING SELECTION-SCREEN 001 WITH WITHHEAD = WITHHEAD
                                                     WITH WITHRINF = WITHRINF
                                                     WITH WITHDD = WITHDD
                                                     WITH LOADDISP = LOADDISP
                                                     WITH WRUN = WRUN
                                                     WITH SYS_LIST = SYS_LIST
                                                     WITH SQLDISP = SQLDISP
                                                     WITH COMDISP = COMDISP
                                                     WITH IGNSTATE = IGNSTATE
                                                     WITH EXTINFO = EXTINFO
                                                     WITH SIMPTRC = SIMPTRC.

ENDFORM.

* build timestamp                                      
FORM BUILD_TIMESTAMP USING TIMESTAMP TYPE INT8
                     CHANGING TIMESTAMP_STR TYPE STRING.
  DATA:
    " time_t type i value timestamp,
    TIME_T_BIAS           TYPE P VALUE '19700101000000', " 1970-01-01 00:00:00
    TSTMP                 TYPE TZNTSTMPL,
    TZONE                 TYPE TZNZONE,
    LOC_TIMESTAMP_STR(32).

  IF TIMESTAMP EQ 0.
    CLEAR TIMESTAMP_STR.
  ELSE.
    CL_ABAP_TSTMP=>ADD(
      EXPORTING
        TSTMP   = TIME_T_BIAS " UTC Time Stamp
        SECS    = TIMESTAMP   " Time Interval in Seconds
      RECEIVING
        R_TSTMP = TSTMP       " UTC Time Stamp
    ).
*    " catch CX_PARAMETER_INVALID_RANGE.                               
*    " catch CX_PARAMETER_INVALID_TYPE.                               

    TZONE = SY-ZONLO. " seems to be the user time zone

    WRITE TSTMP TIME ZONE TZONE TO LOC_TIMESTAMP_STR.
    TIMESTAMP_STR = LOC_TIMESTAMP_STR(19).
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

        C_LOAD(10),
        C_QUEUE(10),
        SNAPSHOTS                   TYPE I,
        SIGNAL_HANDLER_TIMEOUTS     TYPE I,
        NUM_LOST_QUEUE_DATAGRAMS    TYPE I,
        PRIO_NORMAL_WITH_HIGH_LOAD  TYPE I,

        CPU_LOAD_OF_LAST_MIN        TYPE F,
        CPU_LOAD_OF_LAST_MIN_DEC    TYPE P LENGTH 6 DECIMALS 3.

  DATA: BEGIN OF QUEUE_INFO_TABL OCCURS 10.
          INCLUDE STRUCTURE QUEUE_INFO.
  DATA: END OF QUEUE_INFO_TABL.

  "CONSTANTS: header_col1_span TYPE i VALUE 40.

  IF SYSTEM_WIDE_LIST EQ 'X'.
    CREATE OBJECT LR_GRID.

    CL_SALV_FORM_CONTENT=>CLEAR( ).
    ROW = 1.

    LR_GRID->CREATE_LABEL(
      ROW    = ROW
      COLUMN = 1
      "colspan = header_col1_span
      TEXT   = TEXT-154 ).

    WRITE SY-UZEIT USING EDIT MASK '__:__:__' TO TIMESTR.
    WRITE SY-DATUM DD/MM/YYYY TO DATESTR.
    LINE = TEXT-155.
    REPLACE '&1' IN LINE WITH DATESTR.
    REPLACE '&2' IN LINE WITH TIMESTR.
    CONDENSE LINE.

    LR_GRID->CREATE_LABEL(
      ROW    = ROW
      COLUMN = 2
      TEXT   = LINE ).

    CL_SALV_FORM_CONTENT=>SET( LR_GRID ).

    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  " Get detailed header infos

    CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_WP_GET_INFO

    ROW    = ROW
    COLUMN = 2
    TEXT   = LINE ).

      LOAD5 = QUEUE_INFO_TABL-LA5.
      LOAD15 = QUEUE_INFO_TABL-LA15.
    ELSE.
      LOAD1 = QUEUE_INFO_TABL-LA1_AS_PERCENT.
      LOAD5 = QUEUE_INFO_TABL-LA5_AS_PERCENT.
      LOAD15 = QUEUE_INFO_TABL-LA15_AS_PERCENT.
    ENDIF.

    IF ( LOAD1 = 0 AND
         LOAD5 = 0 AND
         LOAD15 = 0 ).

    ELSE.

      WRITE LOAD1 TO C_LOAD1.
      WRITE LOAD5 TO C_LOAD5.
      WRITE LOAD15 TO C_LOAD15.
      IF LOADDISP <> ' '.
        CONDENSE C_LOAD1.

        CONDENSE C_LOAD15.

      IF LOAD_INFO <> DP_NORMAL_LOAD.
        " get wp type specific load info

      ENDIF.

                            CHANGING ETD_LAST_EVENT_TIME_STRING.
    LINE = TEXT-181.
    WRITE ETD_EVENTS TO C_NUMBER.
    REPLACE '&' IN LINE WITH C_NUMBER.
    REPLACE '&' IN LINE WITH ETD_LAST_EVENT_TIME_STRING.
    CONDENSE LINE.

    LR_GRID->CREATE_LABEL(
      ROW    = ROW
      COLUMN = 2
      TEXT   = LINE ).
    ROW = ROW + 1.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

************************************************************************                              &nb

FORM BUILD_FIELDCAT USING FIELDCAT TYPE SLIS_T_FIELDCAT_ALV

  LAYOUT-ALLOW_SWITCH_TO_LIST = 'X'.
ENDFORM.                    "BUILD_LAYOUT

  LAYOUT-BOX_FIELDNAME = 'SELECTED'.

  LAYOUT-ALLOW_SWITCH_TO_LIST = 'X'.

************************************************************************                              &nb
* build alv excluded fcodes for sm50                               &nb
************************************************************************                              &nb
FORM BUILD_EXTAB USING EXTAB TYPE SLIS_T_EXTAB.
  IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X'.
    APPEND 'TRCK' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRCDOWNLD' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRCDONWLDA' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRCF' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRCF_OLD' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRLC' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRDC' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRRSALL' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRRSWP' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRDP' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'DP' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRCDPDL' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'TRRSDP' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'DUMP' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'STOP' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'RYES' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'RNO' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'CLEAR' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'CLEAR_ERR' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'BUFFR' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'BUFF1' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'BUFF0' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'WPDI' TO EXTAB.
    APPEND 'WPDI_DC' TO EXTAB.
    "APPEND 'SYS_PROC' TO extab.
    APPEND 'USER' TO EXTAB.
  ENDIF.

  IF SY-DBSYS <> 'DB6'.
    APPEND 'DBDET' TO EXTAB.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "BUILD_LAYOUT

************************************************************************                              &nb
* handle user commands of sm50                                 &n
************************************************************************                              &nb
##CALLED
FORM SM50_USER_CMD USING R_UCOMM LIKE SY-UCOMM
                         RS_SELFIELD TYPE SLIS_SELFIELD.
  DATA: WP_SELECTED LIKE TH_BOOL,
        LOC_R_UCOMM LIKE SY-UCOMM.

  WP_SELECTED = TH_FALSE.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV WHERE SELECTED = 'X'.
    WP_SELECTED = TH_TRUE.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

  " allow 'fast' selection for some commands
  IF WP_SELECTED = TH_FALSE AND
    (  R_UCOMM = 'WPDI_DC' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'TRCF'
    ).
    IF RS_SELFIELD-TABINDEX > 0.
      READ TABLE WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV INDEX RS_SELFIELD-TABINDEX.
      IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
        WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
        MODIFY WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV INDEX RS_SELFIELD-TABINDEX.
        WP_SELECTED = TH_TRUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  " Special opcode to display priority of running requests
  IF R_UCOMM = 'EXTEND_HEADER'.
    IF EXTEND_HEADER = ABAP_FALSE.
      EXTEND_HEADER = ABAP_TRUE.
    ELSE.
      EXTEND_HEADER = ABAP_FALSE.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  " for functions which needs a worker selection: check now
  IF WP_SELECTED = TH_FALSE AND
     ( R_UCOMM = 'STOP' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'DUMP' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'RYES' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'RNO'  OR
       R_UCOMM = 'TRCF' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'TRCF_OLD' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'DBDET' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'TRCDOWNLD' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'DEL'  OR
       R_UCOMM = 'DEBU' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'EXIT' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'USER' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'WPDI_DC' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'TINFO' OR
       R_UCOMM = 'C-STACK' OR

       " el 10Jan2013 - require wp selection before calling the detail info screen
       "
       " At the moment we have problems when selection a running work process inside
       " the detail information screen. Therefore we don't allow to enter the detail
       " info screen without a selected workprocess.
       R_UCOMM = 'WPDI'
     ).
    MESSAGE S101.
  ELSE.
    CASE R_UCOMM.
      WHEN 'STOP'.
        PERFORM WP_STOP.
      WHEN 'DUMP'.
        PERFORM WP_DUMP.
      WHEN 'RYES'.
        PERFORM WP_RYES.
      WHEN 'RNO'.
        PERFORM WP_RNO.
      WHEN 'TRCK'.
        PERFORM WP_TRACE_COMP USING WP_SELECTED.
      WHEN 'TRCF'.
        PERFORM WP_TRACE_DISPLAY USING TH_TRUE.
      WHEN 'TRCF_OLD'.
        PERFORM WP_TRACE_DISPLAY USING TH_FALSE.
        "TODO delete
      WHEN 'DBDET'.
        PERFORM WP_DB_AGENT_DISPLAY.
      WHEN 'TRCDOWNLD'.
        PERFORM WP_TRACE_DOWNLOAD.
      WHEN 'TRCDOWNLDA'.
        SUBMIT RSMON000_DOWNLOAD_TRACES VIA SELECTION-SCREEN AND RETURN.
      WHEN 'TRCDPDL'.
        PERFORM WP_DPTRACE_DOWNLOAD.
      WHEN 'TRRSALL'.
        PERFORM WP_RESET_TRACE USING TH_TRUE.
      WHEN 'TRRSWP'.
        PERFORM WP_RESET_TRACE USING TH_FALSE.
      WHEN 'TRRSDP'.
        PERFORM DP_RESET_TRACE.
      WHEN 'TRLC'.
        CALL FUNCTION 'STRC_SELECT_LOADMASK'.
      WHEN 'TRDC'.
        CALL FUNCTION 'STRC_SELECT_DISPLAYMASK'.
      WHEN 'TRDP'.
        PERFORM DP_TRACE_LEVEL.
      WHEN 'DP'.
        FILETYPE = 'DP'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'DP.OLD'.
        FILETYPE = 'DP.OLD'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'ICM'.
        FILETYPE = 'ICM'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'ICM.OLD'.
        FILETYPE = 'ICM.OLD'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'MS'.
        FILETYPE = 'MS'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'MS.OLD'.
        FILETYPE = 'MS.OLD'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'RD'.
        FILETYPE = 'RD'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'RD.OLD'.
        FILETYPE = 'RD.OLD'.
        PERFORM DISP_FILE.
      WHEN 'DEL'.
        PERFORM DELETE_SESSION.
      WHEN 'DEBU'.
        PERFORM PROG_DEBU.
      WHEN 'EXIT'.
        PERFORM PROG_EXIT.
      WHEN 'USER'.
        PERFORM SHOW_USER_INFO.
      WHEN 'WPDI'.
        PERFORM WP_SHOW_DETAIL_INFO USING RS_SELFIELD-SEL_TAB_FIELD.
      WHEN 'WPDI_DC'.
        PERFORM WP_SHOW_DETAIL_INFO USING RS_SELFIELD-SEL_TAB_FIELD.
      WHEN 'BUFR'.
        CALL 'ThSysInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_MBUF_REFRESH.
        MESSAGE S116.
      WHEN 'BUF1'.
        CALL 'ThSysInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_MBUF_USE.
        MESSAGE S114.
      WHEN 'BUF0'.
        CALL 'ThSysInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_MBUF_BYPASS.
        MESSAGE S115.
      WHEN 'HELP'.
        PERFORM SHOW_HELP_INFO USING RS_SELFIELD.
      WHEN 'CONF'.
        PERFORM CONFIGURE_SM50 USING 1.
      WHEN 'CLEAR'.
        PERFORM CLEAR_HEADER.
      WHEN 'CLEAR_ERR'.
        PERFORM CLEAR_ERR_COUNTER.
      WHEN 'TINFO'.
        PERFORM SHOW_TINFO.
      WHEN 'C-STACK'.
        PERFORM WRITE_C_STACK.
      WHEN 'SYS_PROC'.
        PERFORM SHOW_SYSTEM_WIDE_LIST.
      WHEN 'RESET_FC'.
        PERFORM RESET_FIELDCAT_IN_DB.
      WHEN 'SNAPSHOT'.
        PERFORM CREATE_SNAPSHOT.
      WHEN 'SNAPSHOTAD'.
        PERFORM ADMINISTER_SNAPSHOTS.
      WHEN 'ACT_WPS'.
        PERFORM SEL_ACTIVE_PROCESSES.
      WHEN 'INFO'.
        CALL FUNCTION 'IWB_HTML_HELP_OBJECT_SHOW'
          EXPORTING
            TCODE  = 'SM50'
          EXCEPTIONS
            OTHERS = 0.
      WHEN 'PIN'.
        SUBMIT RSTRC000 VIA SELECTION-SCREEN AND RETURN.

*- 자동 새로고침 추가                                      
      WHEN 'AUTO'.
        PERFORM AUTO_REFRESH.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDIF.

* Short cut fuer Anzeige der dev_w* und dev_wp* Dateien                          
  IF R_UCOMM(1) = 'W'.
    " TODO: el, 11Sep2017 - Fragment to display sna traces???
    "
    " I guess this is a fragment to display the traces of the SNA worker processes of the
    " gateway. So as they are no longer supported this should be removed.
*    IF r_ucomm(2) = 'WP'.                                &n
*      loc_r_ucomm = r_ucomm+2.                              &nbs
*      PERFORM get_no USING loc_r_ucomm CHANGING wpno.                        &n
*      IF r_ucomm CS '.OLD'.                              &n
*        filetype = 'XG.OLD'.                             &n
*      ELSE.                                   &nb
*        filetype = 'XG'.                              
*      ENDIF.                                   &n
*    ELSE.                                     &nb
    LOC_R_UCOMM = R_UCOMM+1.
    PERFORM GET_NO USING LOC_R_UCOMM CHANGING WPNO.
    IF R_UCOMM CS '.OLD'.
      FILETYPE = 'XP.OLD'.
    ELSE.
      FILETYPE = 'XP'.
    ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.                                     &n
    IF WPNO <> SPACE.
      PERFORM DISP_FILE.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM BUILD_LOCAL_LIST.
  RS_SELFIELD-REFRESH = 'X'.
  RS_SELFIELD-COL_STABLE = 'X'.
  RS_SELFIELD-ROW_STABLE = 'X'.
ENDFORM.                    "SM50_USER_CMD

##CALLED
FORM SM50_SYSTEM_USER_CMD USING R_UCOMM LIKE SY-UCOMM
                                RS_SELFIELD TYPE SLIS_SELFIELD.
  DATA: ACT_INDEX        LIKE SY-TABIX,
        SYSTEM_LIST_LINE TYPE WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV.

  ACT_INDEX = RS_SELFIELD-TABINDEX.
  CLEAR SYSTEM_LIST_LINE.

  " read selected table line
  IF ACT_INDEX > 0.

    READ TABLE SYSTEM_WORKER_LIST_ALV INDEX RS_SELFIELD-TABINDEX INTO SYSTEM_LIST_LINE.

    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
      " allow 'fast' selection for some commands
      IF R_UCOMM = 'REMOTE'.
        SYSTEM_LIST_LINE-SELECTED = 'X'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CASE R_UCOMM.
    WHEN 'REMOTE'.
      IF RS_SELFIELD-SEL_TAB_FIELD = 'WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SESSION_KEY'.
        PERFORM SHOW_USER_IN_SM04 USING SYSTEM_LIST_LINE.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM REMOTE_TRANSACTION USING SYSTEM_LIST_LINE.
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'CONF'.
      PERFORM CONFIGURE_SM50 USING 0.

    WHEN 'ACT_WPS'.
      PERFORM SEL_ACTIVE_PROCESSES.
  ENDCASE.

  " Refresh system list.
  PERFORM BUILD_SYSTEM_LIST.
  RS_SELFIELD-REFRESH = 'X'.
  RS_SELFIELD-COL_STABLE = 'X'.
  RS_SELFIELD-ROW_STABLE = 'X'.
ENDFORM.

FORM SHOW_USER_IN_SM04 USING SYSTEM_LIST_LINE TYPE WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV.
  IF SYSTEM_LIST_LINE-SERVER_NAME = MYNAME.
    SUBMIT (SM04_REPORT) USING SELECTION-SCREEN 001 WITH SESKEY = SYSTEM_LIST_LINE-SESSION_KEY AND RETURN.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'TH_DISPLAY_SM04_ENTRY' DESTINATION SYSTEM_LIST_LINE-SERVER_NAME
      EXPORTING
        SESSION_KEY = SYSTEM_LIST_LINE-SESSION_KEY
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS      = 0.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

FORM REMOTE_TRANSACTION USING SYSTEM_LINE TYPE WORKER_INFO_DISP_ALV.
  DATA: COMM_MESSAGE(256),
        SYST_MESSAGE(256) ##NEEDED ,                        "#EC NEEDED
        DEST TYPE MSNAME2.

  IF SYSTEM_LINE-SELECTED = ' '.
    MESSAGE S101.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  DEST = SYSTEM_LINE-SERVER_NAME.
  IF DEST = MYNAME.
    " If we are here we are already running SM50. So we must have the authority to do that.
    " But in order to get rid of the complaining extended program check we check it again.
    " Check authority for transaction ABAP_DISPATCHER
    CALL FUNCTION 'AUTHORITY_CHECK_TCODE'
      EXPORTING
        TCODE  = `SM50`
      EXCEPTIONS
        NOT_OK = 1
        OTHERS = 0.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    CALL TRANSACTION 'SM50'.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'TH_REMOTE_TRANSACTION'
      EXPORTING
        DEST         = DEST
        TCODE        = 'SM50'
      IMPORTING
        COMM_MESSAGE = COMM_MESSAGE
        SYST_MESSAGE = SYST_MESSAGE.
    IF COMM_MESSAGE <> SPACE.
      " The parameters of the MESSAGE instruction have a maximum
      " length of 50 characters. Therefore, we split comm_message
      " into several substring.
      MESSAGE E102 WITH DEST COMM_MESSAGE(50) COMM_MESSAGE+50(50) COMM_MESSAGE+100(50).
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "remote_transaction

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nbs
*       FORM DISP_FILE                                &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nbs
FORM DISP_FILE.
  DATA: LX_EXCEPTION TYPE REF TO CX_COS_CHECK_FAILED,
        ERROR_TEXT   TYPE STRING.

  ERROR_TEXT = TEXT-309.

  " User is allowed to read traces?
  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ) = ABAP_FALSE.
    MESSAGE S455.
    AUTH_ERR = 1.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  TRY.
      CALL METHOD CL_COS_UTILITIES=>DISABLE
        EXPORTING
          IO_CLIENT    = CL_COS_CLIENT=>SC_APPLICATION_CLIENT
          IV_COMPONENT = ERROR_TEXT.
    CATCH CX_COS_CHECK_FAILED INTO LX_EXCEPTION.
      MESSAGE LX_EXCEPTION TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'I'.
      EXIT.
  ENDTRY.

  CALL SCREEN 1100.
ENDFORM.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nbs
*       FORM show_help_info                               &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nbs
*       ........                                  
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nbs
*  -->  rs_selfield                                   &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nbs
FORM SHOW_HELP_INFO USING RS_SELFIELD TYPE SLIS_SELFIELD.
  DATA: DOKU LIKE DOKHL.

  CASE RS_SELFIELD-FIELDNAME.
    WHEN 'WP_INDEX'.   DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPNO'.
    WHEN 'WP_TYP'.     DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPTYP'.
    WHEN 'PID'.        DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPPID'.
    WHEN 'WP_STATUS'.  DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPSTATUS'.
    WHEN 'WP_WAITING'. DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPWAITING'.
    WHEN 'WP_RESTART'. DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPRESTART'.
    WHEN 'WP_DUMPS'.   DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPDUMPS'.
    WHEN 'WP_SEM'.     DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPSEM'.
    WHEN 'WP_CPU'.     DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPCPU'.
    WHEN 'WP_ELTIME'.  DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPELZEIT'.
    WHEN 'WP_REPORT'.  DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPREPORT'.
    WHEN 'TENANT_DISP'.DOKU-OBJECT = 'MANDT'.
    WHEN 'USER_NAME'.   DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPBNAME'.
    WHEN 'WP_ACTION'.  DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPACTION'.
    WHEN 'WP_TABLE'.   DOKU-OBJECT = 'WPTABLE'.
  ENDCASE.

  CALL FUNCTION 'DOCU_CALL'
    EXPORTING
      DISPL             = 'X'
      DISPL_MODE        = '2'
      ID                = 'DE'
      LANGU             = SY-LANGU
      OBJECT            = DOKU-OBJECT
      SHORTTEXT         = DOKU-OBJECT
      DISPLAY_SHORTTEXT = 'X'
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS            = 0.

ENDFORM.
"SHOW_HELP_INFO
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM configure_sm50                               &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       read configuration for sm50                            &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               

FORM CONFIGURE_SM50 USING LOCAL_LIST TYPE I.
  IN_SM50_CONFIG_SCREEN = ABAP_TRUE.
  IF LOCAL_LIST = 1.
    CALL SELECTION-SCREEN 001 STARTING AT 10 10.
  ELSE.
    CALL SELECTION-SCREEN 002 STARTING AT 10 10.
  ENDIF.
  IN_SM50_CONFIG_SCREEN = ABAP_FALSE.

  PERFORM SET_SM50_CONFIGURATION USING LOCAL_LIST.
ENDFORM.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM CLEAR_HEADER.
  " TODO use other check?
  PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'RESET_TRACE' AUTH_ERR.
  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_WP_CLEAR_HEADER.

ENDFORM.

  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_WP_CLEAR_ERR_COUNTER.

ENDFORM.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM GET_TRACE                                &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       get work process trace level                           &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*ELEL FORM get_trace USING wp_index LIKE wpinfo-wp_index                            &
*               CHANGING trace_level TYPE i.                    &
*                                          &n
*  DATA : t TYPE thfb_trace.                                 
*  CALL FUNCTION 'TH_GET_WP_TRACE'                                 &nb
*    EXPORTING                                     
*      wp_id = wp_index                                
*    IMPORTING                                     
*      trc   = t.                               &n
*                                          &n
*  trace_level = t-level.                                   
*                                          &n
*ENDFORM.                                         

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM DP_TRACE_LEVEL                               &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       Tracelevel des Dispatchers setzen                           &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM DP_TRACE_LEVEL.
  " User is allowed to change the trace?
  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ACTIVITY = CL_SSI_AUTH=>TRC_ACTIVITY_TYPE-CHANGE ) = ABAP_FALSE.
    MESSAGE S457.
    AUTH_ERR = 1.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM GET_DP_TRACE_LEVEL.
  CALL SCREEN 1001 STARTING AT 1 1.
ENDFORM.                    "DP_TRACE_LEVEL

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM CHANGE_DP_TRACE                               &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       change dispatcher trace level                            
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM CHANGE_DP_TRACE.

  DATA: NAME(100),
        NAME_LEN   TYPE I,
        VALUE(100),
        VALUE_LEN  TYPE I.


  NAME = 'rdisp/TRACE'.
  NAME_LEN = STRLEN( NAME ).
  VALUE = TRC_LEVEL.
  VALUE_LEN = STRLEN( VALUE ).
  CALL 'ThSysInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_CHANGE_DISP_PARAM
                   ID 'NAME' FIELD NAME
                   ID 'NAME_LEN' FIELD NAME_LEN
                   ID 'VALUE' FIELD VALUE
                   ID 'VALUE_LEN' FIELD VALUE_LEN.

ENDFORM.                    "CHANGE_DP_TRACE

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM WP_TRACE_COMP                               &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       set trace components and level of work process                      
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM WP_TRACE_COMP USING WP_SELECTED LIKE TH_BOOL.
  DATA : ABORT TYPE ABAP_BOOL.

  " User allowed to change / configure the trace?
  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ACTIVITY = CL_SSI_AUTH=>TRC_ACTIVITY_TYPE-CHANGE ) = ABAP_FALSE.
    MESSAGE S457.
    AUTH_ERR = 1.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'TH_QUERY_WP_TRACE'
    EXPORTING
      WP_ID = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX
      SET   = ' '
    IMPORTING
      RC    = ABORT
    CHANGING
      NTRC  = TRC.

  IF ABORT = ABAP_TRUE.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  IF TRC-WPTYPE <> SPACE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'TH_SET_WP_TRACE'
      EXPORTING
        TRC = TRC.
  ELSE.
    IF WP_SELECTED = TH_FALSE.
      MESSAGE S101.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
      IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
        CALL FUNCTION 'TH_SET_WP_TRACE'
          EXPORTING
            WP_ID = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX
            TRC   = TRC.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "WP_TRACE_COMP

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*      Form  CHECK_UCOMM                                
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       text                                  &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*  -->  p1        text                            &n
*  <--  p2        text                            &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM CHECK_UCOMM.
  CASE SY-UCOMM.
    WHEN 'QUIT'.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.
    WHEN 'BACK'.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.
    WHEN 'CANC'.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.
    WHEN 'UPDA'.
      PERFORM CHANGE_DP_TRACE.
      SET SCREEN 0.
      LEAVE SCREEN.
    WHEN 'DEFA'.
      TRC_LEVEL = 1.
  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.                    "CHECK_UCOMM

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*      Module  PAI_EXIT  INPUT                             &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       text                                  &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
MODULE PAI_EXIT INPUT.
  SET SCREEN 0.
  LEAVE SCREEN.
ENDMODULE.                             " PAI_EXIT  INPUT

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*      Form  WP_TRACE_FILE                               &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       text                                  &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*  -->  p1        text                            &n
*  <--  p2        text                            &n
*----------------------------------------------------------------------                              &nbs
FORM WP_TRACE_FILE.

  DATA: L_VALUE          TYPE STRING,
        L_DATASET_OPENED TYPE ABAP_BOOL.

  ##NEEDED
  DATA : TMP_LISTTYPE TYPE X,
         BEGIN OF REC OCCURS 0,
           LINE(256) TYPE C,
         END OF REC,
         NO(5),
         DUMMY(10),
         THR(64)       TYPE C,
         LINE(132)     TYPE C,
         FILENAME(120) TYPE C,
         WORKDIR(120)  TYPE C,
         DEVFILE(20)   TYPE C,
         LINENR        TYPE I,
         EXTENSION(10) TYPE C,
         OFF           TYPE I,
         THREADS(1)    TYPE C.

  CASE FILETYPE.
    WHEN 'WP'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_w'.
      LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
        IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
          WRITE WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX TO PWP_INDEX LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
          DEVFILE+5  = PWP_INDEX.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF SIMPTRC = ABAP_FALSE.
        CALL FUNCTION 'STRC_DISPLAY_WP_TRACE'
          EXPORTING
            FILE        = DEVFILE
            DISPLAYMASK = 'All'
          EXCEPTIONS
            OTHERS      = 2.
        IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
          MESSAGE S118 WITH DEVFILE.
          "MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE 'I' NUMBER sy-msgno WITH sy-msgv1.
        ENDIF.
        OFF = 2.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    WHEN 'WP_OLD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_w'.
      LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
        IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
          WRITE WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX TO PWP_INDEX LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
          DEVFILE+5  = PWP_INDEX.
          ##NO_TEXT
          CONCATENATE DEVFILE '.old' INTO DEVFILE.
          CONDENSE DEVFILE NO-GAPS.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF SIMPTRC = ABAP_FALSE.
        CALL FUNCTION 'STRC_DISPLAY_WP_TRACE'
          EXPORTING
            FILE        = DEVFILE
            DISPLAYMASK = 'All'
          EXCEPTIONS
            OTHERS      = 2.
        IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
          MESSAGE S118 WITH DEVFILE.
          "MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE 'I' NUMBER sy-msgno WITH sy-msgv1.
        ENDIF.
        OFF = 2.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    WHEN 'DP'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_disp'.
    WHEN 'DP.OLD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_disp.old'.
    WHEN 'ICM'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_icm'.
      THREADS = 'X'.
    WHEN 'ICM.OLD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_icm.old'.
      THREADS = 'X'.
    WHEN 'MS'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_ms'.
    WHEN 'MS.OLD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_ms.old'.
    WHEN 'RD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_rd'.
    WHEN 'RD.OLD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_rd.old'.
    WHEN 'XP'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_w'.
      DEVFILE+5  = WPNO.
      OFF = 2.

      IF SIMPTRC = ABAP_FALSE.
        " Use same display for WP and XP files???
        CALL FUNCTION 'STRC_DISPLAY_WP_TRACE'
          EXPORTING
            FILE        = DEVFILE
            DISPLAYMASK = 'All'
          EXCEPTIONS
            OTHERS      = 2.
        IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
          MESSAGE S118 WITH DEVFILE.
          "MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE 'I' NUMBER sy-msgno WITH sy-msgv1.
        ENDIF.
        OFF = 2.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
        " End???
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'XP.OLD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_w'.
      SPLIT WPNO AT '.' INTO NO DUMMY.
      DEVFILE+5  = NO.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE+10 = '.old'.
      CONDENSE DEVFILE NO-GAPS.
      OFF = 2.

      IF SIMPTRC = ABAP_FALSE.
        " Use same display for WP_OLD and XP.OLD files???
        CALL FUNCTION 'STRC_DISPLAY_WP_TRACE'
          EXPORTING
            FILE        = DEVFILE
            DISPLAYMASK = 'All'
          EXCEPTIONS
            OTHERS      = 2.
        IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.
          MESSAGE S118 WITH DEVFILE.
          "MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE 'I' NUMBER sy-msgno WITH sy-msgv1.
        ENDIF.
        OFF = 2.
        SET SCREEN 0.
        LEAVE SCREEN.
        " End???
      ENDIF.

    WHEN 'XG'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_wp'.
      DEVFILE+6  = WPNO.
    WHEN 'XG.OLD'.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE    = 'dev_wp'.
      SPLIT WPNO AT '.' INTO NO DUMMY.
      DEVFILE+6  = NO.
      ##NO_TEXT
      DEVFILE+11 = '.old'.
      CONDENSE DEVFILE NO-GAPS.
      OFF = 2.
    WHEN OTHERS.
      MESSAGE S118 WITH '?'.
      EXIT.
  ENDCASE.

  SUPPRESS DIALOG.
  LEAVE TO LIST-PROCESSING AND RETURN TO SCREEN 0.

  SET PF-STATUS 'FILE'.
  SET TITLEBAR '300' WITH MYNAME DEVFILE.

  ##NO_TEXT
  CL_SPFL_PROFILE_PARAMETER=>GET_VALUE(
    EXPORTING
      NAME  = 'rdisp/workdir'
    IMPORTING
      VALUE = L_VALUE ).
  WORKDIR = L_VALUE.

  CONDENSE DEVFILE NO-GAPS.

  CALL 'BUILD_DS_SPEC' ID 'PATH'     FIELD WORKDIR
                       ID 'FILENAME' FIELD DEVFILE
                       ID 'OPSYS'    FIELD SY-OPSYS
                       ID 'RESULT'   FIELD FILENAME.

  TRY.
      L_DATASET_OPENED = ABAP_FALSE.
      OPEN DATASET FILENAME IN TEXT MODE FOR INPUT ENCODING DEFAULT.
      IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
        L_DATASET_OPENED = ABAP_TRUE.
      ENDIF.
    CATCH CX_SY_FILE_AUTHORITY.                         "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  IF L_DATASET_OPENED = ABAP_FALSE.

* nicht gefunden, nochmals mit Extension probieren                             &
    CL_SPFL_PROFILE_PARAMETER=>GET_VALUE(
      EXPORTING
        NAME  = 'FT_LOG'
      IMPORTING
        VALUE = L_VALUE ).
    EXTENSION = L_VALUE.

    DEVFILE+10 = EXTENSION.
    CONDENSE DEVFILE NO-GAPS.
    CALL 'BUILD_DS_SPEC' ID 'PATH'     FIELD WORKDIR
                         ID 'FILENAME' FIELD DEVFILE
                         ID 'OPSYS'    FIELD SY-OPSYS
                         ID 'RESULT'   FIELD FILENAME.

    TRY.
        L_DATASET_OPENED = ABAP_FALSE.
        OPEN DATASET FILENAME IN TEXT MODE FOR INPUT ENCODING DEFAULT.
        IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
          L_DATASET_OPENED = ABAP_TRUE.
        ENDIF.
      CATCH CX_SY_FILE_AUTHORITY.                       "#EC NO_HANDLER
    ENDTRY.

    IF L_DATASET_OPENED = ABAP_FALSE.
      MESSAGE S118 WITH FILENAME.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  TMP_LISTTYPE = LISTTYPE.
  LISTTYPE = 2.

  SET BLANK LINES ON.

  LINENR = 0.

  DATA: OFF1 TYPE I,
        OFF2 TYPE I,
        OFF3 TYPE I,
        OFF4 TYPE I,
        OFF5 TYPE I,
        OFF6 TYPE I.

  OFF1 = 3 + OFF.
  OFF2 = 7 + OFF.
  OFF3 = 10 + OFF.
  OFF4 = 19 + OFF.
  OFF5 = 13 + OFF.
  OFF6 = 16 + OFF.

  IF OFF > 0.
    OFF = OFF + 1.
  ENDIF.

  DO.

    READ DATASET FILENAME INTO REC.
    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
    IF THREADS = 'X'.
* copied from RSMONICM_ALV                                   &nbs
      SPLIT REC-LINE AT ']' INTO THR LINE.
* Time stamp ?                                     &nbs
      IF LINE+4(1)  = ' ' AND
         LINE+8(1)  = ' ' AND
         LINE+11(1) = ' ' AND
         LINE+20(1) = ' ' AND
         LINE+14(1) = ':' AND
         LINE+17(1) = ':'.
        WRITE: /  REC-LINE COLOR COL_NORMAL.
      ELSEIF LINE+1(10) = '*** ERROR '.
        WRITE: / REC-LINE COLOR COL_NEGATIVE.
      ELSE.
        WRITE: / REC-LINE.
      ENDIF.
* end copy                                       &
    ELSE.
* Time stamp ?                                     &nbs
      IF REC-LINE+OFF1(1) = ' ' AND
         REC-LINE+OFF2(1) = ' ' AND
         REC-LINE+OFF3(1) = ' ' AND
         REC-LINE+OFF4(1) = ' ' AND
         REC-LINE+OFF5(1) = ':' AND
         REC-LINE+OFF6(1) = ':'.
        WRITE: /  REC-LINE COLOR COL_NORMAL.
      ELSEIF REC-LINE+OFF(10) = '*** ERROR'.
        WRITE: / REC-LINE COLOR COL_NEGATIVE.
      ELSEIF REC-LINE+OFF(6) = '***LOG'.
        WRITE: / REC-LINE COLOR COL_KEY.
      ELSE.
        WRITE : / REC-LINE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    LINENR = LINENR + 1.

  ENDDO.

  CLOSE DATASET FILENAME.
  MESSAGE S112 WITH FILENAME LINENR.

ENDFORM.                               " WP_TRACE_FILE

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*      Module  SHOW_TRC_LEVELS  INPUT                            
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       text                                  &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
MODULE SHOW_TRC_LEVELS INPUT.

  ##DECL_MODUL
  DATA: SELECTED_LEVEL LIKE THTRCLEVEL-TRC_LEVEL.
  ##DECL_MODUL
  DATA: BEGIN OF FIELD_TBL OCCURS 1.
          ##DECL_MODUL
          INCLUDE STRUCTURE HELP_VALUE.
  ##DECL_MODUL
  DATA: END OF FIELD_TBL.

  ##DECL_MODUL
  DATA: BEGIN OF TRCLVL OCCURS 4,
          TRC_LEVEL LIKE THTRCLEVEL-TRC_LEVEL,
          DUMMY     LIKE THTRCLEVEL-TRC_LEVEL,
          TRC_TEXT  LIKE THTRCLEVEL-TRC_TEXT,
        END OF TRCLVL.

  REFRESH TRCLVL.

  TRCLVL-TRC_LEVEL = '0'.
  TRCLVL-DUMMY     = SPACE.
  TRCLVL-TRC_TEXT  = TEXT-212.
  APPEND TRCLVL.

  TRCLVL-TRC_LEVEL = '1'.
  TRCLVL-DUMMY     = SPACE.
  TRCLVL-TRC_TEXT  = TEXT-213.
  APPEND TRCLVL.

  TRCLVL-TRC_LEVEL = '2'.
  TRCLVL-DUMMY     = SPACE.
  TRCLVL-TRC_TEXT  = TEXT-214.
  APPEND TRCLVL.

  TRCLVL-TRC_LEVEL = '3'.
  TRCLVL-DUMMY     = SPACE.
  TRCLVL-TRC_TEXT  = TEXT-215.
  APPEND TRCLVL.

  FREE FIELD_TBL.
  FIELD_TBL-TABNAME    = 'THTRCLEVEL'.
  FIELD_TBL-FIELDNAME  = 'TRC_LEVEL'.
  FIELD_TBL-SELECTFLAG = 'X'.
  APPEND FIELD_TBL.

  CALL FUNCTION 'F4TOOL_F4FUNCTION_BRIDGE'
    EXPORTING
      DISPLAY_ONLY   = SPACE
      FIELDNAME      = SPACE
      TABNAME        = SPACE
    IMPORTING
      SELECTED_VALUE = SELECTED_LEVEL
    TABLES
      VALUE_TAB      = TRCLVL
      FIELDS_TAB     = FIELD_TBL
    EXCEPTIONS
      OTHERS         = 99.

  IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
    TRC_LEVEL = SELECTED_LEVEL.
  ELSE.
    TRC_LEVEL = 1.
  ENDIF.
ENDMODULE.                             " SHOW_TRC_LEVELS  INPUT

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM wp_trace_display                               
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       display work process trace file                           
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM WP_TRACE_DISPLAY USING DISP_NEW LIKE TH_BOOL.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV WHERE SELECTED = 'X'.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF DISP_NEW = TH_TRUE.
    FILETYPE = 'WP'.
  ELSE.
    FILETYPE = 'WP_OLD'.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM DISP_FILE.

ENDFORM.                    "WP_TRACE_DISPLAY



*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM wp_trace_download                               
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       download work process trace file                          &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM WP_TRACE_DOWNLOAD.
  DATA: FILENAME(20)      TYPE C,
        LOC_FILENAME(255) TYPE C,
        LOC_DIR(255)      TYPE C,
        LOC_FILESIZE      TYPE I,
        RC                TYPE I,
        WP_INDEX(10)      TYPE C,
        WITH_DIALOG(1)    TYPE C VALUE 'X',
        ANSWER(1)         TYPE C,
        NO_DOWNLOAD       TYPE I,
        NO_DOWNLOAD_OK    TYPE I,
        NO_DOWNLOAD_ERR   TYPE I.

  " User allowed to read traces?
  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ) = ABAP_FALSE.
    MESSAGE S455.
    AUTH_ERR = 1.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV WHERE SELECTED = 'X'.
    NO_DOWNLOAD = NO_DOWNLOAD + 1.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF NO_DOWNLOAD = 0.
    MESSAGE E409.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.

    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.

* Abfragedialog ausschalten ?                                   &
      IF ANSWER = 'X'.
        WITH_DIALOG = SPACE.
      ENDIF.

* Dateiname der Tracedatei bilden dev_w<x>                              &n
      WP_INDEX = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX.
      ##NO_TEXT
      CONCATENATE 'dev_w' WP_INDEX INTO FILENAME.
      CONDENSE FILENAME NO-GAPS.
      PERFORM DOWNLOAD_FILE USING FILENAME LOC_DIR WITH_DIALOG
                            CHANGING LOC_FILENAME LOC_FILESIZE RC.
      IF RC = 0.

        DATA: POPUP_SEEN(1) TYPE C.

* bestimme Verzeichnis, falls mehr als eine Datei gesichert werden soll                      &n
        IF LOC_DIR = SPACE AND NO_DOWNLOAD > 1.
          DATA: MOFF TYPE I,
                OFF  TYPE I VALUE 0.
          FIND FILENAME IN SECTION OFFSET OFF OF LOC_FILENAME
                    IN CHARACTER MODE
                    MATCH OFFSET MOFF.
          IF MOFF > 2.
            LOC_DIR = LOC_FILENAME(MOFF).
          ELSE.
            LOC_DIR = SPACE.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

* Abfrage ausschalten                                     &n
        IF POPUP_SEEN = SPACE AND NO_DOWNLOAD > 1.
          DATA: QUESTION(255) TYPE C.
          CONCATENATE TEXT-098 ' ' TEXT-099 INTO QUESTION.
          POPUP_SEEN = 'X'.
          CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
            EXPORTING
              TITLEBAR      = TEXT-104
              TEXT_QUESTION = QUESTION
            IMPORTING
              ANSWER        = ANSWER
            EXCEPTIONS
              OTHERS        = 0.
          IF ANSWER = '1'.
            ANSWER = 'X'.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        IF NO_DOWNLOAD = 1.
          MESSAGE I151 WITH LOC_FILENAME LOC_FILESIZE.
        ELSE.
          NO_DOWNLOAD_OK = NO_DOWNLOAD_OK + 1.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.

* DISK_FULL, macht keinen Sinn hier weiterzumachen                             &
        IF RC = 5.
          MESSAGE E159.
        ENDIF.
        IF RC = 6.
          MESSAGE E160.
        ENDIF.

        IF NO_DOWNLOAD = 1.
          CASE RC.
            WHEN 1.
              MESSAGE W152 WITH FILENAME.
            WHEN 3.
              MESSAGE W153 WITH FILENAME.
            WHEN 2.
              MESSAGE W154.
            WHEN 4.
              MESSAGE W155.
            WHEN 7.
              MESSAGE W161 WITH FILENAME.
          ENDCASE.
        ELSE.
          NO_DOWNLOAD_ERR = NO_DOWNLOAD_ERR + 1.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.

  IF NO_DOWNLOAD > 1.
    IF NO_DOWNLOAD_ERR > 0.
      MESSAGE I157 WITH NO_DOWNLOAD_OK NO_DOWNLOAD_ERR.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE I158 WITH NO_DOWNLOAD_OK.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "WP_TRACE_DISPLAY

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM wp_dptrace_download                              &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       download work process trace file                          &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM WP_DPTRACE_DOWNLOAD.
  ##NO_TEXT
  DATA: FILENAME(20)      TYPE C VALUE 'dev_disp',
        LOC_FILENAME(255) TYPE C,
        LOC_FILESIZE      TYPE I,
        RC                TYPE I.

  " User is allowed to read traces?
  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ) = ABAP_FALSE.
    MESSAGE S455.
    AUTH_ERR = 1.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM DOWNLOAD_FILE USING FILENAME SPACE 'X'
                        CHANGING LOC_FILENAME LOC_FILESIZE RC.

  IF RC = 0.
    MESSAGE I151 WITH LOC_FILENAME LOC_FILESIZE.
  ELSE.
    CASE RC.
      WHEN 1.
        MESSAGE W152 WITH FILENAME.
      WHEN 3.
        MESSAGE W153 WITH FILENAME.
      WHEN 2.
        MESSAGE W154.
      WHEN 4.
        MESSAGE W155.
      WHEN 5.
        MESSAGE E159.
      WHEN 6.
        MESSAGE E160.
      WHEN 7.
        MESSAGE E161 WITH FILENAME.
      WHEN OTHERS.
        MESSAGE W156.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "wp_dptrace_download

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM DOWNLOAD_FILE                               &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM DOWNLOAD_FILE USING    FILENAME TYPE C
                            DIRECTORY TYPE C
                            WITH_DIALOG TYPE C
                   CHANGING LOC_FILENAME TYPE C
                            LOC_FILESIZE TYPE I
                            RC       TYPE I.

  DO.
    CALL FUNCTION 'TH_DOWNLOAD_TEXTFILE'
      EXPORTING
        FILENAME             = FILENAME
        DIRECTORY            = DIRECTORY
        WITH_DIALOG          = WITH_DIALOG
      IMPORTING
        ACT_FILENAME         = LOC_FILENAME
        ACT_FILESIZE         = LOC_FILESIZE
      EXCEPTIONS
        FILE_OPEN_ERROR      = 1
        USER_CANCELED        = 2
        FILE_WRITE_ERROR     = 3
        NO_AUTHORITY         = 4
        DISK_FULL            = 5
        NOT_SUPPORTED_BY_GUI = 6
        ACCESS_DENIED        = 7
        OTHERS               = 99.
    RC = SY-SUBRC.

    IF RC = 5.
      DATA: QUESTION(255) TYPE C, ANSWER(1) TYPE C.
      CONCATENATE TEXT-100 TEXT-101 INTO QUESTION SEPARATED BY SPACE.
      CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
        EXPORTING
          TITLEBAR      = TEXT-102
          TEXT_QUESTION = QUESTION
        IMPORTING
          ANSWER        = ANSWER
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 0.
      IF ANSWER <> '1'.
        RC = 5.
        EXIT.
      ELSE.
* ok, nochmal probieren                                    &
* leider wird loc_filename wegen der Execption nicht geliefert                         &nb
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    EXIT.
  ENDDO.

ENDFORM.                    "DOWNLOAD_FILE

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM WP_RESET_TRACE                               &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       reset trace files                               
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM WP_RESET_TRACE USING RESET_ALL LIKE TH_BOOL.
  DATA: MY_ANSWER(1).

  " User is allowed to reset/delete trace files?
  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ACTIVITY = CL_SSI_AUTH=>TRC_ACTIVITY_TYPE-RESET ) = ABAP_FALSE.
    MESSAGE S456.
    AUTH_ERR = 1.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR MY_ANSWER.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
    EXPORTING
      TITLEBAR              = TEXT-059
      TEXT_QUESTION         = TEXT-058
      DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '1'
      DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
    IMPORTING
      ANSWER                = MY_ANSWER.

  IF MY_ANSWER <> '1'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  IF RESET_ALL = TH_TRUE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'TH_RESET_TRACE'
      EXPORTING
        ALL_RESET = 'X'
        DP_RESET  = 'X'
        GW_RESET  = 'X'
        RFC_RESET = 'X'.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'TH_RESET_TRACE'.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "WP_RESET_TRACE

  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ACTIVITY = CL_SSI_AUTH=>TRC_ACTIVITY_TYPE-RESET ) = ABAP_FALSE.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
* FORM GET_DP_TRACE_LEVEL                                    &nbs
* Use new AD-API (W.Rehm 14.04.2015)                                &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM GET_DP_TRACE_LEVEL.

  DATA: VALUE          TYPE STRING,
        RC             TYPE SY-SUBRC,
        LO_ADM_MESSAGE TYPE REF TO CL_ADM_MESSAGE,
        SNAME          TYPE SADM_SERVERNAME.

  SNAME = CL_ABAP_SYST=>GET_INSTANCE_NAME( ).

  CREATE OBJECT LO_ADM_MESSAGE.

  LO_ADM_MESSAGE->SET_EXECUTION( IF_ADM_EXECUTION_TYPES=>AD_DISPATCHER ).
  RC = LO_ADM_MESSAGE->AD_PROFILE( EXPORTING SERVERNAME = SNAME
                                             NAME       = 'rdisp/TRACE'
                                             TIMEOUT    = 1000
                                   IMPORTING VALUE      = VALUE ).

*----------------------------------------------------------------------                              &nbs
* Parameterwerte holen                                     &
*----------------------------------------------------------------------                              &nbs
  IF RC = 0.
    TRC_LEVEL = VALUE.
  ELSE.
    TRC_LEVEL = '1'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    "GET_DP_TRACE_LEVEL

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*      Module  PAI_1001  INPUT                             &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       text                                  &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
MODULE PAI_1001 INPUT.
  PERFORM CHECK_UCOMM.
ENDMODULE.                    "PAI_1001 INPUT

*func*****************************************************************                               
* Funktion     :AUTH_CHECK                                 &
* Autor        :Edgar Lott                              
* Datum        :12.08.92                               
* Beschreibung :                                      &
*                                          &n
* falls notwendig, Berechtigung ueberpruefen                                
*                                          &n
* Korrekturen  :                                     &n
*end******************************************************************                               
FORM AUTH_CHECK USING UCOMM TYPE C AUTH_ERR TYPE X.
  AUTH_ERR = 0.

  IF ( UCOMM <> 'END' ) AND
     ( UCOMM <> 'BACK' ) AND
     ( UCOMM <> 'ABBR' ) AND
     ( UCOMM <> 'WPDI' ) AND
     ( UCOMM <> 'WPDI_DC' ) AND
     ( UCOMM <> 'CPU' ) AND
     ( UCOMM <> 'USER' ) AND
     ( UCOMM <> 'REFR' ).
    AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'S_ADMI_FCD'
      ID 'S_ADMI_FCD' FIELD 'PADM'.
    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE S433.
      AUTH_ERR = 1.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "AUTH_CHECK


*func*****************************************************************                               
* Funktion     :WP_STOP / WP_DUMP                              &n
* Autor        :Edgar Lott                              
* Datum        :12.08.92                               
* Parameter    :                                   &nbs
* Ergebnis     :                                   
* Seiteneffekte:                                       
* Lokale Daten :                                     &n
* Globale Daten:                                      &
* DB-Tabellen  :                                     &n
* Beschreibung :                                      &
*                                          &n
* WP_STOP:   Workprozess abbrechen                                &nbs
*                                          &n
* WP_DUMP:   Workprozess mit Dump abbrechen                             &nb
*                                          &n
* WP_RYES:   Restart auf ja setzen                               
*                                          &n
* WP_RNO:    Restart auf nein setzen                              
*                                          &n
* DELETE_SESSION:  Benutzermodus loeschen                                &n
*                                          &n
* PROG_DEBU: Programm in Debugging-Modus versetzen                              
*                                          &n
* PROG_EXIT: Programm abbrechen                                   
*                                          &n
* Korrekturen  :                                     &n
*end******************************************************************                               
FORM WP_STOP.
  DATA: MY_ANSWER(1),
        CONFIRM      LIKE TH_BOOL VALUE TH_TRUE,
        LOC_PID      TYPE WPPID.

  PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'STOP' AUTH_ERR.
  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
      IF CONFIRM = TH_TRUE.
        CONFIRM = TH_FALSE.
        CLEAR MY_ANSWER.

        CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
          EXPORTING
            TITLEBAR              = TEXT-048
            TEXT_QUESTION         = TEXT-047
            DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
            DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
          IMPORTING
            ANSWER                = MY_ANSWER.

        IF MY_ANSWER <> '1'.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      " TODO create method with new types
      LOC_PID = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-PID.
      CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_WP_STOP
                      ID 'PID' FIELD LOC_PID.
      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
        MESSAGE S435 WITH TEXT-041.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    "WP_STOP

FORM CREATE_SNAPSHOT.
  DATA: MY_ANSWER(1),
        SERVER_NAME TYPE SSI_SERVERNAME.

  " User is allowed to change traces
  IF CL_SSI_AUTH=>IS_TRC_ACCESS_ALLOWED( ACTIVITY = CL_SSI_AUTH=>TRC_ACTIVITY_TYPE-CHANGE ) = ABAP_FALSE.
    MESSAGE S457.
    AUTH_ERR = 1.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
    EXPORTING
      TITLEBAR              = TEXT-061
      TEXT_QUESTION         = TEXT-060
      DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
      DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
    IMPORTING
      ANSWER                = MY_ANSWER.
  IF MY_ANSWER <> '1'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  SERVER_NAME = MYNAME.
  TRY.
      CL_SERVER_ADMIN=>CREATE_SNAPSHOT( SERVER_NAME = SERVER_NAME ).
    CATCH CX_SSI_NO_AUTH.
      MESSAGE E150 WITH TEXT-540.
  ENDTRY.
  MESSAGE S509 WITH SERVER_NAME.

ENDFORM.                    "WP_STOP

FORM ADMINISTER_SNAPSHOTS.
  SUBMIT (SNAPSHOT_REPORT) AND RETURN.
ENDFORM.

FORM SEL_ACTIVE_PROCESSES.
  IF WRUN = ' '.
    WRUN = 'X'.
  ELSE.
    WRUN = ' '.
  ENDIF.
  PERFORM SET_ACT_WPS_TEXT USING WRUN.
ENDFORM.

FORM WP_RYES.

  PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'RYES' AUTH_ERR.
  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
      IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-RESTART <> 1.
        CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_WP_RESTART_ON
                        ID 'WP_INDEX' FIELD WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    "WP_RYES

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM WP_RNO.

  PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'RNO' AUTH_ERR.
  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
      IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-RESTART <> 0.
        CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_WP_RESTART_ON
                        ID 'WP_INDEX' FIELD WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    "WP_RNO

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM DELETE_SESSION                               &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*                                          &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM DELETE_SESSION.
  DATA: MY_ANSWER(1),
        CONFIRM         LIKE TH_BOOL VALUE TH_TRUE,
        LOC_SESSION_HDL TYPE INT1.

  PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'DEL' AUTH_ERR.
  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
      IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X' AND WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-TENANT_DISP <> SY-MANDT.
        MESSAGE S665.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF CONFIRM = TH_TRUE.
        CONFIRM = TH_FALSE.
        CLEAR MY_ANSWER.

        CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
          EXPORTING
            TITLEBAR              = TEXT-050
            TEXT_QUESTION         = TEXT-049
            DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
            DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
          IMPORTING
            ANSWER                = MY_ANSWER.

        IF MY_ANSWER <> '1'.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      " TODO create kernel method with new types
      LOC_SESSION_HDL = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SESSION_HDL.
      CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE'   FIELD OPCODE_WP_DEL
                      ID 'WP_INDEX' FIELD WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX
                      ID 'USER_ID'     FIELD WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-LOGON_ID
                      ID 'SESSION_HDL' FIELD LOC_SESSION_HDL.

      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
        MESSAGE ID '14' TYPE 'S' NUMBER SY-SUBRC WITH ''.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    "DELETE_SESSION

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM PROG_DEBU                                &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*                                          &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM PROG_DEBU.
  DATA: MY_ANSWER(1),
        CONFIRM         LIKE TH_BOOL VALUE TH_TRUE,
        LOC_SESSION_HDL TYPE INT1.

  PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'DEBU' AUTH_ERR.
  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
      IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X' AND WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-TENANT_DISP <> SY-MANDT.
        MESSAGE S665.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF CONFIRM = TH_TRUE.
        CONFIRM = TH_FALSE.

        CLEAR MY_ANSWER.

        CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
          EXPORTING
            TITLEBAR              = TEXT-052
            TEXT_QUESTION         = TEXT-051
            DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
            DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
          IMPORTING
            ANSWER                = MY_ANSWER.

        IF MY_ANSWER <> '1'.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      " TODO create kernel method with new types
      LOC_SESSION_HDL = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SESSION_HDL.
      CALL FUNCTION 'TH_DEBUG_WP'
        EXPORTING
          WP_INDEX                    = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX
          LOGON_ID                    = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-LOGON_ID
          SESSION_HDL                 = LOC_SESSION_HDL
        EXCEPTIONS
          NO_AUTHORITY                = 1
          TOO_MANY_DEBUGGING_SESSIONS = 2
          OTHERS                      = 3.

      IF SY-SUBRC = 1.
        MESSAGE S433.
        EXIT.
      ELSEIF SY-SUBRC = 2.
        MESSAGE S111.
        EXIT.
      ELSEIF SY-SUBRC = 3.
        MESSAGE S134.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    "PROG_DEBU

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM PROG_EXIT                                &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*                                          &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM PROG_EXIT.
  DATA: MY_ANSWER(1),
        CONFIRM         LIKE TH_BOOL VALUE TH_TRUE,
        LOC_SESSION_HDL TYPE INT1.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
      IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X' AND WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-TENANT_DISP <> SY-MANDT.
        MESSAGE S665.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF CONFIRM = TH_TRUE.
        CONFIRM = TH_FALSE.
        PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'EXIT' AUTH_ERR.
        IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR MY_ANSWER.

        CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
          EXPORTING
            TITLEBAR              = TEXT-054
            TEXT_QUESTION         = TEXT-053
            DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
            DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
          IMPORTING
            ANSWER                = MY_ANSWER.

        IF MY_ANSWER <> '1'.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      " TODO create kernel method with new types
      LOC_SESSION_HDL = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SESSION_HDL.
      CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE'      FIELD OPCODE_WP_EXIT
                      ID 'WP_INDEX'    FIELD WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX
                      ID 'LOGON_ID'    FIELD WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-LOGON_ID
                      ID 'SESSION_HDL' FIELD LOC_SESSION_HDL.
      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
        MESSAGE S435 WITH TEXT-042.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    "PROG_EXIT

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM show_user_info                               &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*                                          &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM SHOW_USER_INFO.
  DATA: SEL_WP LIKE WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV,
        FOUND  LIKE TH_BOOL.

  PERFORM GET_SELECTED_WP CHANGING SEL_WP FOUND.
  IF FOUND = TH_FALSE.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'SUSR_SHOW_USER_DETAILS'
    EXPORTING
      MANDT = SEL_WP-TENANT_DISP
      BNAME = SEL_WP-USER_NAME.
ENDFORM.                    "SHOW_USER_INFO

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM get_selected_wp                               &
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*                                          &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*  -->  sel_wp                                    &n
*  -->  found                                    &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM GET_SELECTED_WP CHANGING SEL_WP STRUCTURE WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV
                              FOUND LIKE TH_BOOL.

  FOUND = TH_FALSE.
  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV WHERE SELECTED = 'X'.
    IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X' AND
       WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-TENANT_DISP <> SY-MANDT.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.
    FOUND = TH_TRUE.
    SEL_WP = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    EXIT.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    "GET_SELECTED_WP

" Show detailed info
FORM WP_SHOW_DETAIL_INFO USING FIELD TYPE SLIS_SEL_TAB_FIELD.
  DATA: SEL_WP LIKE WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV,
        FOUND  LIKE TH_BOOL.

  CLEAR EXTAB_FOR_DETAIL.

  " Don't show detail informations if this is not a system client
  IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X'.
    MESSAGE S168.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  SEL_WP_INDEX = -1.

  PERFORM GET_SELECTED_WP CHANGING SEL_WP FOUND.
  IF FOUND = TH_TRUE.
    SEL_WP_INDEX =  SEL_WP-WP_INDEX.
    APPEND 'DETAIL' TO EXTAB_FOR_DETAIL.
  ENDIF.

  IF FIELD = 'WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SESSION_KEY' AND FOUND = TH_TRUE.
    SUBMIT (SM04_REPORT) USING SELECTION-SCREEN 001 WITH SESKEY = SEL_WP-SESSION_KEY AND RETURN.
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  IF WITHDD = ' '.
    CALL SCREEN 2000.
  ELSE.
    CALL SCREEN 3000.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    "WP_SHOW_DETAIL_INFO

" Show technical info
FORM SHOW_TINFO.
  DATA: ROW_COUNT TYPE I.

  PERFORM BUILD_TINFO_TAB.

  " it could be that no work process infos have been selected as all selected
  " work process belong to another client
  " this happens with active content separation
  DESCRIBE TABLE TINFO_TAB_ALV LINES ROW_COUNT.
  IF ROW_COUNT = 0.
    MESSAGE S664.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM BUILD_TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT USING TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT[].
  PERFORM BUILD_TINFO_TAB_LAYOUT USING TINFO_TAB_LAYOUT.
  PERFORM BUILD_TINFO_TAB_EVENT USING TINFO_TAB_EVENTS[].

  IS_VARIANT-HANDLE = 'INFO'.
  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_GRID_DISPLAY'
    EXPORTING
      I_CALLBACK_PROGRAM       = SY-REPID
      I_CALLBACK_PF_STATUS_SET = 'SM50_TINFO_TAB_SET_STATUS'
      I_CALLBACK_USER_COMMAND  = 'SM50_TINFO_TAB_USER_CMD'
      I_SAVE                   = 'A'
      IT_FIELDCAT              = TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT[]
      IS_LAYOUT                = TINFO_TAB_LAYOUT
      IS_VARIANT               = IS_VARIANT
      IT_EVENTS                = TINFO_TAB_EVENTS[]
    TABLES
      T_OUTTAB                 = TINFO_TAB_ALV.

  " if there are less infos than selected work processes
  " some processes belong to other clients and shall not be displayed
  IF ROW_COUNT < SELECTED_COUNTER.
    MESSAGE S664.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

FORM BUILD_TINFO_TAB.
  DATA: COUNTER TYPE I.

  REFRESH TINFO_TAB.
  COUNTER = 0.
  SELECTED_COUNTER = 0.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.

      SELECTED_COUNTER = SELECTED_COUNTER + 1.

      IF IS_SYSTEM_CLIENT <> 'X' AND WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-TENANT_DISP <> SY-MANDT.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      COUNTER = COUNTER + 1.
      IF COUNTER > 1.
        CLEAR TINFO_TAB-FIELD.
        CLEAR TINFO_TAB-VALUE.
        APPEND TINFO_TAB.
      ENDIF.

      CALL 'ThWpInfo' ID 'OPCODE' FIELD OPCODE_WP_TINFO_LIST
            ID 'WP_HANDLE' FIELD WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX
            ID 'TABLE' FIELD TINFO_TAB[].
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  REFRESH TINFO_TAB_ALV.
  LOOP AT TINFO_TAB.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING TINFO_TAB TO TINFO_TAB_ALV.
    CLEAR TINFO_TAB_ALV-LINE_COL.

    IF TINFO_TAB_ALV-FIELD = ' '.
      TINFO_TAB_ALV-FIELD = TEXT-149.
      TINFO_TAB_ALV-LINE_COL = 'C30'.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND TINFO_TAB_ALV.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.

FORM BUILD_TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT USING TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT TYPE
SLIS_T_FIELDCAT_ALV.
  DATA: LS_FIELDCAT TYPE SLIS_FIELDCAT_ALV.

  REFRESH TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT.

  CLEAR LS_FIELDCAT.
  LS_FIELDCAT-FIELDNAME    = 'FIELD'.
  LS_FIELDCAT-KEY          = 'X'.
  LS_FIELDCAT-TABNAME      = 'TINFO_TAB'.
  LS_FIELDCAT-OUTPUTLEN    = 30.
  LS_FIELDCAT-REPTEXT_DDIC = TEXT-147.
  APPEND LS_FIELDCAT TO TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT.

  CLEAR LS_FIELDCAT.
  LS_FIELDCAT-FIELDNAME    = 'VALUE'.
  LS_FIELDCAT-TABNAME      = 'SES_TAB'.
  LS_FIELDCAT-OUTPUTLEN    = 70.
  LS_FIELDCAT-REPTEXT_DDIC = TEXT-148.
  APPEND LS_FIELDCAT TO TINFO_TAB_FIELDCAT.
ENDFORM.

FORM BUILD_TINFO_TAB_LAYOUT USING LAYOUT TYPE SLIS_LAYOUT_ALV.
*  LAYOUT-NO_VLINE      = 'X'.                              
*  LAYOUT-F2CODE        = 'HOT'.                            
*  LAYOUT-BOX_FIELDNAME = 'SELECTED'.                                 
*  LAYOUT-ALLOW_SWITCH_TO_LIST = 'X'.                                 

  CLEAR LAYOUT.

  LAYOUT-INFO_FIELDNAME = 'LINE_COL'.
  LAYOUT-COLTAB_FIELDNAME = 'FIELD_COL'.
ENDFORM.

  RS_SELFIELD-ROW_STABLE = 'X'.

  SET PF-STATUS 'TINFO_TAB' EXCLUDING RT_EXTAB.

*  clear c_event.                                     &
