ENDFORM.

AT USER-COMMAND.
  "ELEL sy-lsind = sy-lsind - 1.

AT LINE-SELECTION.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM GET_NO                                &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       String -> Integer                              &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM GET_NO USING STR TYPE SY-UCOMM CHANGING NO TYPE C.
  DATA: LEN         TYPE I, ONE_CHAR(1).
  NO = SPACE.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
* Pr#fe erstes Zeichen                                    &n
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
  ONE_CHAR = STR.
  IF NOT ( ONE_CHAR CA '0123456789' ).
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
* Durchsuche den ganzen String ob ung#ltige Zeichen vorkommen                         &nbs
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
  LEN = STRLEN( STR ).
  LEN = LEN - 1.
  DO LEN TIMES.
    ONE_CHAR = STR+SY-INDEX.
    IF NOT ( ONE_CHAR CA '0123456789' ).
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDDO.
  NO = STR.
ENDFORM.                    "GET_NO

" Write C-Stack and ABAP-Stack of selected work processes
" (except the own one)
FORM WRITE_C_STACK.
  PERFORM AUTH_CHECK USING 'C-STACK' AUTH_ERR.
  IF AUTH_ERR <> 0.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  DATA: SERVER_INFO TYPE REF TO CL_SERVER_INFO.
  DATA: OWN_WORKER_INFO TYPE REF TO CL_WORKER_INFO.
  DATA: WORKER_INFO TYPE REF TO CL_WORKER_INFO.
  DATA: MY_ANSWER(1).

  CLEAR MY_ANSWER.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM'
    EXPORTING
      TITLEBAR              = TEXT-063
      TEXT_QUESTION         = TEXT-062
      DEFAULT_BUTTON        = '2'
      DISPLAY_CANCEL_BUTTON = ' '
    IMPORTING
      ANSWER                = MY_ANSWER.

  IF MY_ANSWER <> '1'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  TRY.
      CREATE OBJECT SERVER_INFO.
      CREATE OBJECT OWN_WORKER_INFO.
    CATCH CX_SSI_NO_AUTH.
      MESSAGE E150 WITH TEXT-540.
      "MESSAGE ID 14 TYPE 'E' NUMBER 150 WITH text-540.
  ENDTRY.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
    IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-SELECTED = 'X'.
      IF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX <> OWN_WORKER_INFO->INDEX.
        TRY.
            CREATE OBJECT WORKER_INFO
              EXPORTING
                INDEX = WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV-WP_INDEX.
            CALL METHOD WORKER_INFO->WRITE_WORKER_STACK( ).
          CATCH CX_SSI_NO_AUTH.
            MESSAGE E150 WITH TEXT-540.
            "MESSAGE ID 14 TYPE 'E' NUMBER 150 WITH text-540.
        ENDTRY.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.


* class definition for time tools                                 
CLASS TIME_TOOLS DEFINITION CREATE PRIVATE FINAL.
  PUBLIC SECTION.

    CLASS-METHODS:
      CONVERT_TIME_2_USEC_TIME
        IMPORTING TIME            TYPE FLOAT
        RETURNING VALUE(TIME_STR) TYPE SSI_USEC_TIME,
      CONVERT_TIME_2_SEC_TIME
        IMPORTING TIME            TYPE FLOAT
        RETURNING VALUE(TIME_STR) TYPE SSI_USEC_TIME.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS TIME_TOOLS IMPLEMENTATION.

  METHOD CONVERT_TIME_2_USEC_TIME.
    DATA: USEC        TYPE I,
          SEC         TYPE I,
          MIN         TYPE I,
          HOUR        TYPE I,
          USEC_STR(6),
          SEC_STR(2),
          MIN_STR(2),
          HOUR_STR(4),
          VALUE(64),
          OFFSET      TYPE I,
          MAX         TYPE I.

    " Time value range exceeded ?
    IF TIME < 0.
      TIME_STR = TEXT-550.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    USEC = TIME MOD 1000000.
    SEC = TIME DIV 1000000.
    MIN = SEC DIV 60.
    SEC = SEC MOD 60.
    HOUR = MIN DIV 60.
    MIN = MIN MOD 60.

    USEC_STR = '000000'.
    MAX = 100000.
    OFFSET = 0.
    WHILE USEC < MAX.
      OFFSET = OFFSET + 1.
      MAX = MAX / 10.
    ENDWHILE.

    WRITE USEC TO USEC_STR+OFFSET LEFT-JUSTIFIED.

    SEC_STR = '00'.
    OFFSET = 0.
    IF SEC < 10.
      OFFSET = 1.
    ENDIF.
    WRITE SEC TO SEC_STR+OFFSET LEFT-JUSTIFIED.

    IF MIN < 10.
      MIN_STR = '0'.
      WRITE MIN TO MIN_STR+1 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ELSE.
      WRITE MIN TO MIN_STR LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ENDIF.

    IF HOUR < 10.
      HOUR_STR = '0'.
      WRITE HOUR TO HOUR_STR+1 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ELSE.
      WRITE HOUR TO HOUR_STR LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE HOUR_STR ':' MIN_STR ':' SEC_STR '.' USEC_STR INTO VALUE.
    TIME_STR = VALUE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CONVERT_TIME_2_SEC_TIME.
    DATA: SEC         TYPE I,
          MIN         TYPE I,
          HOUR        TYPE I,
          SEC_STR(2),
          MIN_STR(2),
          HOUR_STR(4),
          VALUE(64),
          OFFSET      TYPE I.

    SEC = TIME MOD 60.
    MIN = TIME DIV 60.
    SEC = SEC MOD 60.
    HOUR = MIN DIV 60.
    MIN = MIN MOD 60.

    SEC_STR = '00'.
    OFFSET = 0.
    IF SEC < 10.
      OFFSET = 1.
    ENDIF.
    WRITE SEC TO SEC_STR+OFFSET LEFT-JUSTIFIED.

    IF MIN < 10.
      MIN_STR = '0'.
      WRITE MIN TO MIN_STR+1 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ELSE.
      WRITE MIN TO MIN_STR LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ENDIF.

    IF HOUR < 10.
      HOUR_STR = '0'.
      WRITE HOUR TO HOUR_STR+1 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ELSE.
      WRITE HOUR TO HOUR_STR LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE HOUR_STR ':' MIN_STR ':' SEC_STR INTO VALUE.
    TIME_STR = VALUE.
  ENDMETHOD.

ENDCLASS. " time_tools



* Class Implementation                                     &

CLASS WORKER_LIST_HANDLER IMPLEMENTATION.

  METHOD CONV_LIST_FOR_DISPLAY.
    DATA: WORKER_INFO_DISP TYPE SSI_WORKER_INFO_DISP,
          WORKER_INFO      TYPE SSI_WORKER_INFO,
          TOTAL_CPU_TIME   TYPE SSI_CPU_TIME.

    CLEAR WORKER_LIST_DISP.

    LOOP AT WORKER_LIST INTO WORKER_INFO.
      CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING WORKER_INFO TO WORKER_INFO_DISP.

      " Translate state into string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-STATE_DISP = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_STATE_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-STATE ).

      " Translate state info into string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-STATE_INFO_DISP = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_STATE_INFO_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-STATE_INFO ).

      " Translate restart into string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-RESTART_DISP = MAP_RESTART_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-RESTART ).

      "Map failues to string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-FAILURES_DISP = MAP_FAILURES_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-FAILURES ).

      " Translate locked semaphore info into string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-SEM_LOCKED = MAP_SEM_LOCKED_2_STR( WORKER_INFO ).

      " Translate locking semaphore info into string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-SEM_LOCKING = MAP_SEM_LOCKING_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKING ).

      " Map elapsed time to string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-ELAPSED_TIME_DISP = MAP_ELAPSED_TIME_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-ELAPSED_TIME ).

      " Translate CPU (sytem+user, system, user) into string
      TOTAL_CPU_TIME = WORKER_INFO-CPU_SYSTEM + WORKER_INFO-CPU_USER.
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-CPU = TIME_TOOLS=>CONVERT_TIME_2_SEC_TIME( TOTAL_CPU_TIME ).
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-CPU_SYSTEM = TIME_TOOLS=>CONVERT_TIME_2_SEC_TIME( WORKER_INFO-CPU_SYSTEM ).
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-CPU_USER = TIME_TOOLS=>CONVERT_TIME_2_SEC_TIME( WORKER_INFO-CPU_USER ).
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-CPU_RAW = TOTAL_CPU_TIME.

      " Translate priority into string
      "
      " As priorities are only relevant for dialog wps, we don't show priorities for other
      " workprocess types.
      IF ( WORKER_INFO-PRIORITY <> RQ_Q_PRIO_INVALID ) AND
         ( WORKER_INFO-WP_TYPE = DP_WORKER_DIAWP ).
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-PRIORITY_DISP = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_PRIORITY_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-PRIORITY ).
      ELSE.
        CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP-PRIORITY_DISP.
      ENDIF.
      IF ( WORKER_INFO-WAIT_FOR_PRIORITY <> RQ_Q_PRIO_INVALID ) AND
         ( WORKER_INFO-WP_TYPE = DP_WORKER_DIAWP ).
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-WAIT_FOR_PRIORITY_DISP = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_PRIORITY_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-WAIT_FOR_PRIORITY ).
      ELSE.
        CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP-WAIT_FOR_PRIORITY_DISP.
      ENDIF.

      " Translate logon type and logon sub type into string.
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-SESSION_TYPE = MAP_SESSION_TYPE_2_STR( WORKER_INFO ).

      " Build session key
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-SESSION_KEY = BUILD_SESSION_KEY( WORKER_INFO ).

      " Map worker action to string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-CURRENT_ACTION_DISP = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_ACTION_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-CURRENT_ACTION ).

      WORKER_INFO_DISP-TENANT_DISP = WORKER_INFO-TENANT.

      " Map worker type to string
      WORKER_INFO_DISP-WP_TYPE_DISP = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_TYPE_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-WP_TYPE ).

      " If this is an restricted view (e.g. not running in system tenant): hide some fields
      IF RESTRICTED_VIEW = '1' AND
         WORKER_INFO-STATE <> WP_WAIT AND
         WORKER_INFO_DISP-TENANT_DISP <> SY-MANDT.
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-WP_PROGRAM = TEXT-122.
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-MAIN_PROGRAM = TEXT-122.
        CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP-TENANT_DISP.
        CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP-USER_NAME.
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-LOGON_HDL = -1.
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-LOGON_ID = -1.
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-SESSION_HDL = -1.
        CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP-SESSION_KEY.
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-CURRENT_ACTION_DISP = TEXT-122.
        CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP-ACTION_INFO.
      ENDIF.

      IF WORKER_INFO-RECONNECT_STATE = CL_RECONNECT=>RECONNECT_IN_PROGRESS.
        WORKER_INFO_DISP-RECONNECT_STATE = 'DB reconnect pending'(125) ##TEXT_DIFF.
      ELSE.
        CLEAR WORKER_INFO_DISP-RECONNECT_STATE.
      ENDIF.

      APPEND WORKER_INFO_DISP TO WORKER_LIST_DISP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Map restart to string
  METHOD MAP_RESTART_2_STR.
    CASE RESTART.
      WHEN 0. RESTART_STR = TEXT-007.
      WHEN 1. RESTART_STR = TEXT-008.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Map locked semaphores to str
  METHOD MAP_SEM_LOCKED_2_STR.
    DATA: TMP1(32),
          TMP2(32).

    CLEAR SEM_LOCKED_STR.
    IF WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_1 <> NO_SEM.
      WRITE WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_1 TO SEM_LOCKED_STR LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ENDIF.
    IF WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_2 <> NO_SEM.
      TMP1 = SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      WRITE WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_2 TO TMP2 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
      CONCATENATE TMP1 ',' TMP2 INTO SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      CONDENSE SEM_LOCKED_STR.
    ENDIF.
    IF WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_3 <> NO_SEM.
      TMP1 = SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      WRITE WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_3 TO TMP2 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
      CONCATENATE TMP1 ',' TMP2 INTO SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      CONDENSE SEM_LOCKED_STR.
    ENDIF.
    IF WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_4 <> NO_SEM.
      TMP1 = SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      WRITE WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_4 TO TMP2 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
      CONCATENATE TMP1 ',' TMP2 INTO SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      CONDENSE SEM_LOCKED_STR.
    ENDIF.
    IF WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_5 <> NO_SEM.
      TMP1 = SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      WRITE WORKER_INFO-SEM_LOCKED_5 TO TMP2 LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
      CONCATENATE TMP1 ',' TMP2 INTO SEM_LOCKED_STR.
      CONDENSE SEM_LOCKED_STR.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Map semaphore locking into string
  METHOD MAP_SEM_LOCKING_2_STR.
    IF SEM_LOCKING <> NO_SEM.
      WRITE SEM_LOCKING TO SEM_LOCKING_STR LEFT-JUSTIFIED.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR SEM_LOCKING_STR.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD MAP_SESSION_TYPE_2_STR.

    DATA: SESSION_SUBTYPE_STR TYPE STRING.

    SESSION_TYPE_STR = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_LOGON_TYPE_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-LOGON_TYPE ).

    IF WORKER_INFO-LOGON_TYPE = DP_LOGON_INTERNAL OR
       "worker_info-logon_type = dp_logon_gui OR
       WORKER_INFO-LOGON_TYPE = DP_LOGON_RFC OR
       WORKER_INFO-LOGON_TYPE = DP_LOGON_PLUGIN OR
       WORKER_INFO-LOGON_TYPE = DP_LOGON_APC.

      SESSION_SUBTYPE_STR = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_LOGON_SUBTYPE_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-LOGON_SUB_TYPE ).
      IF SESSION_SUBTYPE_STR IS NOT INITIAL.
        SESSION_TYPE_STR = SESSION_SUBTYPE_STR.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  " Map elapsed time to string
  METHOD MAP_ELAPSED_TIME_2_STR.
    IF ELAPSED_TIME > 0.
      WRITE ELAPSED_TIME TO ELAPSED_TIME_STR RIGHT-JUSTIFIED.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD MAP_FAILURES_2_STR.
    IF FAILURES > 0.
      WRITE FAILURES TO FAILURES_STR.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD BUILD_SESSION_KEY.
    DATA: TMP       TYPE STRING,
          TOSTR(64).

    IF WORKER_INFO-LOGON_HDL = NO_LOGON_HDL.
      CLEAR SESSION_KEY.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    TMP = 'T%_U%_M%'.
    TOSTR = WORKER_INFO-LOGON_HDL.
    REPLACE '%' IN TMP WITH TOSTR.
    TOSTR = WORKER_INFO-LOGON_ID.
    REPLACE '%' IN TMP WITH TOSTR.
    TOSTR = WORKER_INFO-SESSION_HDL.
    REPLACE '%' IN TMP WITH TOSTR.
    CONDENSE TMP NO-GAPS.
    SESSION_KEY = TMP.
  ENDMETHOD.

*    ENDCASE.

* Class Definitions for detail info of worker processes                          &nbs
CLASS DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER DEFINITION CREATE PRIVATE FINAL.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    CONSTANTS: SET_NEW_SELECTION    TYPE I VALUE 1,
               KEEP_SELECTION       TYPE I VALUE 2,

               HEIGHT_OF_TOP_MANY   TYPE I VALUE 30,
               HEIGHT_OF_TOP_SINGLE TYPE I VALUE 10.

    CLASS-DATA:
      SCREEN TYPE REF TO DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER.

    CLASS-METHODS:
      CREATE_SCREEN
        IMPORTING WORKER_SELECTION TYPE I
                  WORKER_INDEX     TYPE SSI_WORKER_INDEX,
      CONTINUE_SCREEN,
      GET_CONTINUE_SCREEN
        RETURNING VALUE(VALUE) TYPE I.

CLASS DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER IMPLEMENTATION.

  METHOD CREATE_SCREEN.
    DATA: WP_VIEW_CHANGED      TYPE I VALUE 0,
          WP_SELECTION_CHANGED TYPE I VALUE 0.

    IF SCREEN IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT SCREEN
        EXPORTING
          WORKER_INDEX = WORKER_INDEX.
    ELSE.
      IF ( WORKER_INDEX = -1 AND SCREEN->SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1 ) OR
         ( WORKER_INDEX <> -1 AND SCREEN->SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 0 ).
        WP_VIEW_CHANGED = 1.
      ELSEIF WORKER_INDEX <> -1 AND WORKER_INDEX <> SCREEN->SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX.
        WP_SELECTION_CHANGED = 1.
      ENDIF.

      IF WP_SELECTION_CHANGED = 1.
        SCREEN->SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX = WORKER_INDEX.
      ENDIF.

      IF WP_VIEW_CHANGED = 1 OR WP_SELECTION_CHANGED = 1.
        SCREEN->REFRESH_WORKLIST_VIEW = 1.
        IF WP_VIEW_CHANGED = 1.
          IF SCREEN->SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
            SCREEN->SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 0.
            SCREEN->SPLITTER_MAIN->SET_ROW_HEIGHT(
              EXPORTING
                ID     = 1
                HEIGHT = HEIGHT_OF_TOP_MANY ).
          ELSE.
            SCREEN->SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
            SCREEN->SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX = WORKER_INDEX.
            SCREEN->SPLITTER_MAIN->SET_ROW_HEIGHT(
              EXPORTING
                ID     = 1
                HEIGHT = HEIGHT_OF_TOP_SINGLE ).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        SCREEN->GET_WORKER_LIST( ).
        SCREEN->INIT_WP_SELECTION( WORKER_INDEX = WORKER_INDEX ).
        SCREEN->FILL_TABLES( ).
        SCREEN->SHOW_WORKER_LIST( EXPORTING FIRST_DISPLAY = 1 ).
      ENDIF.
      SCREEN->CONT_SCREEN = 1.
    ENDIF.

    IF WORKER_SELECTION = SET_NEW_SELECTION.
      SCREEN->INIT_WP_SELECTION( WORKER_INDEX = WORKER_INDEX ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.                    "create_screen

  METHOD CONTINUE_SCREEN ##NEEDED .
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_CONTINUE_SCREEN.
    IF SCREEN IS INITIAL.
      VALUE = 0.
    ELSE.
      VALUE = SCREEN->GET_CONT_SCREEN( ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SET_CONT_SCREEN.
    CONT_SCREEN = VALUE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_CONT_SCREEN.
    VALUE = CONT_SCREEN.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CONSTRUCTOR.

          L_HEIGHT_OF_TOP     TYPE I.

    IF WORKER_INDEX <> -1.

    " Register events for toolbar

    SET HANDLER ME->ON_DOUBLE_CLICK_OF_WORKER_LIST FOR WORKER_VIEWER.

    IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 0.

  METHOD INIT_WP_SELECTION.
    DATA: ROW TYPE I.

    ROW = GET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_INDEX( EXPORTING INDEX = WORKER_INDEX ).
    IF ROW <> -1.
      SET_WORKER_SELECTION_IN_LIST( ROW = ROW ).
    ENDIF.
    IF WORKER_INDEX <> -1 AND ROW <> -1.
      ACTIVE_DETAIL_CONTROL = 'RUNTIME_INFO'.
      SET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_ROW( ROW = ROW ).
      REFRESH_DETAIL_CONTAINER( ).
    ELSEIF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
      SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX = WORKER_INDEX.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.                    "constructor

  METHOD FILL_TOOLBAR.

    CALL METHOD TOOLBAR->ADD_BUTTON(

  METHOD ON_FUNCTION_SELECTED.

    IF FCODE = 'VIEW'.
      TOGGLE_TO_COMPACT_VIEW( ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'SINGLE'.
      IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_CONTROL <> FCODE.
        " Need to recreate the single container.
        REBUILD_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
      ENDIF.

      ACTIVE_DETAIL_CONTROL = FCODE.
      CASE FCODE.
        WHEN 'RUNTIME_INFO'.
          " IF withrinf = ' '.
          "   MESSAGE s673. Don't display nasty message
          " ENDIF.
          SHOW_RUNTIME_INFO( ).
        WHEN 'DB'.
          SHOW_DB_STATISTIC( ).
        WHEN 'MEMORY'.
          SHOW_MEMORY_STATISTIC( ).
        WHEN 'APPLICATION'.
          SHOW_APPLICATION_INFO( ).
        WHEN 'ATTRIBUTES'.
          SHOW_ATTRIBUTES( ).
        WHEN 'SQL'.
          SHOW_SQL( ).
      ENDCASE.
    ELSE.
      ACTIVE_DETAIL_CONTROL = FCODE.
      TOGGLE_VIEW( ).
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD REBUILD_SINGLE_VIEW.
    DELETE_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
    CREATE_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD TOGGLE_VIEW.
    REFRESH_WORKLIST_VIEW = 1.
    IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'COMPACT'.
      TOGGLE_TO_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
    ELSE.
      TOGGLE_TO_COMPACT_VIEW( ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD TOGGLE_TO_SINGLE_VIEW.
    DELETE_COMPACT_VIEW( ).

    ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'SINGLE'.
    IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_CONTROL IS INITIAL.
      ACTIVE_DETAIL_CONTROL = 'RUNTIME_INFO'.
    ENDIF.
    CREATE_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
    REFRESH_DETAIL_CONTAINER( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD TOGGLE_TO_COMPACT_VIEW.
    IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'COMPACT'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    DELETE_SINGLE_VIEW( ).

    ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'COMPACT'.
    CREATE_COMPACT_VIEW( ).
    REFRESH_DETAIL_CONTAINER( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

*  METHOD handle_worker_double_click.                                  
*    DATA: "ELEL worker_info             TYPE ssi_worker_info,               &nb

  " Not used at the momebnt
  "Extend toolbar of worker list
  "METHOD on_toolbar_for_worker_list.
  "  data: l_wa_button type stb_button.

  "  l_wa_button-function = 'REFRESH'.
  "  l_wa_button-icon = ICON_REFRESH.

  "METHOD on_user_command_of_worker_list.
  "ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD ON_DOUBLE_CLICK_OF_WORKER_LIST.
    REFRESH_ALL( WORKER_SELECTION = SET_NEW_SELECTION ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  " Refresh worker list if selection is not SET_NEW_SELECTION
  " read new detail tables
  " refresh detail containers
  METHOD REFRESH_ALL.
    REFRESH_WORKER_LIST( WORKER_SELECTION = WORKER_SELECTION ).
    FILL_TABLES( ).
    REFRESH_DETAIL_CONTAINER( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_ROW.
    DATA: WORKER_INFO TYPE SSI_WORKER_INFO.

    READ TABLE WORKER_LIST INDEX ROW INTO WORKER_INFO.
    IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
      SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX = WORKER_INFO-WP_INDEX.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD INVALIDATE_WORKER_SELECTION.
    SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX = -1.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SET_WORKER_SELECTION.
    SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX = INDEX.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SET_WORKER_SELECTION_IN_LIST.
    DATA: SELECTED_ROWS     TYPE LVC_T_ROW,
          SELECTED_ROW_LINE TYPE LVC_S_ROW.

    IF ROW = -1.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    SELECTED_ROW_LINE-INDEX = ROW.
    APPEND SELECTED_ROW_LINE TO SELECTED_ROWS.
    WORKER_VIEWER->SET_SELECTED_ROWS( IT_INDEX_ROWS = SELECTED_ROWS ).
    SET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_ROW( EXPORTING ROW = ROW ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_WORKER_SELECTION_FROM_LIST.
    DATA: SELECTED_ROWS        TYPE LVC_T_ROW,
          SELECTED_ROW_LINE    TYPE LVC_S_ROW,
          NUM_OF_SELECTED_ROWS TYPE I.

    WORKER_VIEWER->GET_SELECTED_ROWS( IMPORTING ET_INDEX_ROWS = SELECTED_ROWS ).
    DESCRIBE TABLE SELECTED_ROWS LINES NUM_OF_SELECTED_ROWS.
    ROW = -1.
    IF NUM_OF_SELECTED_ROWS > 0.
      LOOP AT SELECTED_ROWS INTO SELECTED_ROW_LINE.
        ROW = SELECTED_ROW_LINE-INDEX.
        RETURN.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
    RETURN.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CLEAR_WORKER_SELECTION_IN_LIST.
    DATA: SELECTED_ROWS TYPE LVC_T_ROW.

    CLEAR SELECTED_ROWS.
    WORKER_VIEWER->SET_SELECTED_ROWS( IT_INDEX_ROWS = SELECTED_ROWS ).
  ENDMETHOD.


  METHOD GET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_INDEX.
    FIELD-SYMBOLS: <WORKER_INFO> TYPE SSI_WORKER_INFO.

    ROW = -1.
    IF INDEX = -1.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT WORKER_LIST ASSIGNING <WORKER_INFO>.
      IF <WORKER_INFO>-WP_INDEX = INDEX.
        ROW = SY-TABIX.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SET_MESSAGE.
    IF SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX = -1.
      MESSAGE S669.
    ELSE.
      MESSAGE S670 WITH SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR_TABLES( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_WORKER_LIST.
    DATA: TITLE(64),
          SELECTED_WORKER_STR(10).

    " el 13Juli2015 - deactivate this code
    "
    " At the moment the reason for this code is quite unclear. But as this always
    " results in an addtion control when switching from the main to the detailed
    " list this code has been deactivated.
    "IF refresh_worklist_view = 1.
    "  worker_viewer->free( ).
    "  CLEAR worker_viewer.
    "  CREATE OBJECT worker_viewer
    "    EXPORTING
    "      i_parent = container_top.
    "ENDIF.

    WORKER_LIST_DISP =
      WORKER_LIST_HANDLER=>CONV_LIST_FOR_DISPLAY(
      EXPORTING
        WORKER_LIST     = WORKER_LIST
        RESTRICTED_VIEW = RESTRICTED_VIEW ).

    IF FIRST_DISPLAY = 1 OR REFRESH_WORKLIST_VIEW = 1.
      DATA: L_LIST_LAYOUT_FOR_WORKER TYPE LVC_S_LAYO.

      REFRESH_WORKLIST_VIEW = 0.

      IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
        " Build title
        WRITE SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX TO SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
        TITLE = TEXT-437.
        REPLACE '&' IN TITLE WITH SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
        CONDENSE TITLE.
        L_LIST_LAYOUT_FOR_WORKER-GRID_TITLE = TITLE.
      ELSE.
        L_LIST_LAYOUT_FOR_WORKER-GRID_TITLE = TEXT-434.
      ENDIF.
      L_LIST_LAYOUT_FOR_WORKER-SMALLTITLE = ' '. "small_title.
      L_LIST_LAYOUT_FOR_WORKER-CWIDTH_OPT = 'X'.    "the column width is adjusted,
      L_LIST_LAYOUT_FOR_WORKER-NO_TOOLBAR = 'X'.

      WORKER_VIEWER->SET_TABLE_FOR_FIRST_DISPLAY(
        EXPORTING
          I_STRUCTURE_NAME = 'SSI_WORKER_INFO_DISP'
          IS_LAYOUT        = L_LIST_LAYOUT_FOR_WORKER
        CHANGING
          IT_OUTTAB        = WORKER_LIST_DISP
          IT_FIELDCATALOG  = WORKER_LIST_DISP_FC
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS           = 0 ).
    ELSE.
      WORKER_VIEWER->REFRESH_TABLE_DISPLAY( ).
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

    FC_LINE-DATATYPE  = 'SSI_WORKER_ATTRIBUTE'.
    FC_LINE-JUST      = 'L'.

    APPEND FC_LINE TO ATTRIBUTE_TAB_FC.

    APPEND FC_LINE TO ATTRIBUTE_TAB_FC.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_WORKER_LIST.
    DATA: SERVER_INFO TYPE REF TO CL_SERVER_INFO.

    CLEAR WORKER_LIST.

    TRY.
        CREATE OBJECT SERVER_INFO
          EXPORTING
            SERVER_NAME = SPACE.

        IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
          WORKER_LIST = SERVER_INFO->GET_WORKER_LIST( WITH_APPLICATION_INFO = 1
                                                      WITH_CPU              = 1
                                                      WORKER_INDEX          = SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX ).
        ELSE.
          WORKER_LIST = SERVER_INFO->GET_WORKER_LIST( WITH_APPLICATION_INFO = 1
                                                      WITH_CPU              = 1
                                                      ONLY_ACTIVE_WORKER    = 1 ).
        ENDIF.
      CATCH CX_SSI_NO_AUTH.
        MESSAGE E150 WITH TEXT-540.
        "MESSAGE ID 14 TYPE 'E' NUMBER 150 WITH text-540.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD FILL_RUNTIME_BUFFER.
    CASE WORKER_INFO-STATE.
      WHEN 4. " worker runs
        GET_STACK( ).

      WHEN 8. " worker holds
        GET_WAITINFO( WORKER_INFO ).
    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD FILL_TABLES.
    DATA: ROW         TYPE I VALUE -1,
          WORKER_INFO TYPE SSI_WORKER_INFO.

    ROW = GET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_INDEX( SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX ).
    IF ROW = -1.
      SET_MESSAGE( ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE WORKER_LIST INDEX ROW INTO WORKER_INFO.
    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      SET_MESSAGE( ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    FILL_RUNTIME_BUFFER( WORKER_INFO ).
    GET_DB_STATISTIC( ).
    GET_MEMORY_STATISTIC( WORKER_INFO ).
    GET_APPLICATION_INFO( ).
    GET_ATTRIBUTES( ).
    GET_SQL( WORKER_INFO ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CLEAR_TABLES.
    CLEAR ABAP_STACK_TAB.
    CLEAR WAITINFO_TAB.
    CLEAR DB_STATISTIC_TAB.
    CLEAR MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.
    CLEAR APPLICATION_INFO_TAB.
    CLEAR ATTRIBUTE_TAB.
    CLEAR SQL_TAB.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD  SHOW_RUNTIME_INFO.
    DATA: WORKER_INFO TYPE SSI_WORKER_INFO,
          ROW         TYPE I.

    ROW = GET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_INDEX( SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX ).
    IF ROW = -1.
      SET_MESSAGE( ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE WORKER_LIST INDEX ROW INTO WORKER_INFO.
    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      SET_MESSAGE( ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    CASE WORKER_INFO-STATE.
      WHEN 4. " runs
        IF ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO <> 'STACK' AND
           ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO IS NOT INITIAL.

          " Recreate runtime view
          " TODO Doesn't work
          IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'SINGLE'.
            REBUILD_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
          ELSE.
            REBUILD_RUNTIME_VIEW( ).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO = 'STACK'.
        SHOW_STACK( ).

      WHEN 8. " holds
        IF ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO <> 'WAIT_INFO' AND
           ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO IS NOT INITIAL.
          " TODO Doesn't work
          IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'SINGLE'.
            REBUILD_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
          ELSE.
            REBUILD_RUNTIME_VIEW( ).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO = 'WAIT_INFO'.
        SHOW_WAITINFO( ).

      WHEN OTHERS.
        IF ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO <> 'EMPTY' AND
           ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO IS NOT INITIAL.

          " Recreate runtime view
          " TODO Doesn't work
          IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'SINGLE'.
            REBUILD_SINGLE_VIEW( ).
          ELSE.
            REBUILD_RUNTIME_VIEW( ).
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

        ACTIVE_RUNTIME_INFO = 'EMPTY'.
        SHOW_DEACTIVATED_RUNTIMEINFO( ).

    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD REBUILD_RUNTIME_VIEW.
    DATA: LV_CONTAINER_RUNTIME TYPE REF TO CL_GUI_CONTAINER.

    IF SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT IS INITIAL OR
      SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.



    IF CONTAINER_RUNTIME IS NOT INITIAL.
      CONTAINER_RUNTIME->FREE( ).
    ENDIF.
    SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM->FREE( ).

    LV_CONTAINER_RUNTIME = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 2 COLUMN = 1 ).
    CREATE OBJECT SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM
      EXPORTING
        PARENT  = LV_CONTAINER_RUNTIME
        ROWS    = 1
        COLUMNS = 1.

    SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM->SET_COLUMN_WIDTH(
      EXPORTING
        ID    = 1
        WIDTH = 28 ).

    CONTAINER_RUNTIME = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 1 ).

    CLEAR STACK_VIEWER.
    CLEAR WAITINFO_VIEWER.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_DEACTIVATED_RUNTIMEINFO.
    DATA: L_LIST_LAYOUT           TYPE LVC_S_LAYO,
          TITLE(64),
          SELECTED_WORKER_STR(10).

    IF STACK_VIEWER IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT STACK_VIEWER
        EXPORTING
          I_PARENT = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    ENDIF.

    " Build title
    IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
      TITLE = 'Work process is not active'(538).         "#EC TEXT_DIFF
    ELSE.
      WRITE SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX TO SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      TITLE = 'Work process & is not active'(539).       "#EC TEXT_DIFF
      REPLACE '&' IN TITLE WITH SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      CONDENSE TITLE.
    ENDIF.

    L_LIST_LAYOUT-GRID_TITLE = TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-SMALLTITLE = SMALL_TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-CWIDTH_OPT = 'X'.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_TOOLBAR = NO_TOOLBAR.
    " Also hide the table header row if there is no runtime info to display
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_HEADERS = 'X'.

    " Just display some empty table (i.e., only show the built title)
    CLEAR ABAP_STACK_TAB.
    STACK_VIEWER->SET_TABLE_FOR_FIRST_DISPLAY(
      EXPORTING
        I_STRUCTURE_NAME = 'SSI_ABAP_STACK_INFO'
        IS_LAYOUT        = L_LIST_LAYOUT
      CHANGING
        IT_OUTTAB        = ABAP_STACK_TAB
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS           = 0 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_STACK.
    DATA: L_LIST_LAYOUT           TYPE LVC_S_LAYO,
          TITLE(64),
          SELECTED_WORKER_STR(10).

    IF STACK_VIEWER IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT STACK_VIEWER
        EXPORTING
          I_PARENT = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    ENDIF.

    " Build title
    IF WITHRINF = 'X'.
      IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
        TITLE = TEXT-534.
      ELSE.
        WRITE SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX TO SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
        TITLE = TEXT-435.
        REPLACE '&' IN TITLE WITH SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
        CONDENSE TITLE.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      TITLE = 'Display of ABAP stack is deactivated'(537). "#EC TEXT_DIFF
    ENDIF.

    L_LIST_LAYOUT-GRID_TITLE = TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-SMALLTITLE = SMALL_TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-CWIDTH_OPT = 'X'.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_TOOLBAR = NO_TOOLBAR.
    " Also hide the table header row if the ABAP stack cannot be displayed
    IF WITHRINF = 'X'.
      L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_HEADERS = ' '.
    ELSE.
      L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_HEADERS = 'X'.
    ENDIF.

    " Always use set_table_for_first display to get auto fitting column sizes
    STACK_VIEWER->SET_TABLE_FOR_FIRST_DISPLAY(
      EXPORTING
        I_STRUCTURE_NAME = 'SSI_ABAP_STACK_INFO'
        IS_LAYOUT        = L_LIST_LAYOUT
      CHANGING
        IT_OUTTAB        = ABAP_STACK_TAB
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS           = 0 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_STACK.

    DATA: NUM_OF_LINES    TYPE I,
          STACK_TAB       TYPE ABAP_EXTCALLSTACK,
          ABAP_STACK_LINE TYPE SSI_ABAP_STACK_INFO.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS: <STACK_LINE>      TYPE ABAP_EXTCALLSTACK_LINE.

    CLEAR ABAP_STACK_TAB.

    IF WITHRINF = ' '.
      " MESSAGE s673. Don't display nasty message
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    " try to read the stack of the selected worker
    TRY.
        CALL FUNCTION 'SYSTEM_CALLSTACK_OF_PROCESS'
          EXPORTING
            WP_ID         = SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX
            MAX_WAIT_TIME = 2000
          IMPORTING
            STACKTAB      = STACK_TAB.
        ##NO_HANDLER
      CATCH CX_STACKOFWP_NO_AUTHORITY.
        ##NO_HANDLER
      CATCH CX_STACKOFWP_WP_NOT_RUN.
        ##NO_HANDLER
      CATCH CX_STACKOFWP_INVALID_WP.
        ##NO_HANDLER
      CATCH CX_STACKOFWP_NOSTACK_AVAIL.
    ENDTRY.

    DESCRIBE TABLE STACK_TAB LINES NUM_OF_LINES.
    IF NUM_OF_LINES = 0.
      MESSAGE S666.
    ELSE.
      LOOP AT STACK_TAB ASSIGNING <STACK_LINE>.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING <STACK_LINE> TO ABAP_STACK_LINE.
        APPEND ABAP_STACK_LINE TO ABAP_STACK_TAB.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_WAITINFO.
    DATA: L_LIST_LAYOUT           TYPE LVC_S_LAYO,
          TITLE(64),
          SELECTED_WORKER_STR(10).

    IF WAITINFO_VIEWER IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT WAITINFO_VIEWER
        EXPORTING
          I_PARENT = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    ENDIF.

    " Build title
    IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
      TITLE = TEXT-535.
    ELSE.
      WRITE SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX TO SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      TITLE = TEXT-436.
      REPLACE '&' IN TITLE WITH SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      CONDENSE TITLE.
    ENDIF.

    L_LIST_LAYOUT-GRID_TITLE = TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-SMALLTITLE = SMALL_TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-CWIDTH_OPT = 'X'.    "the column width is adjusted,
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_TOOLBAR = NO_TOOLBAR.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_HEADERS = ' '.

    " Always use set_table_for_first display to get auto fitting column sizes
    WAITINFO_VIEWER->SET_TABLE_FOR_FIRST_DISPLAY(
      EXPORTING
        I_STRUCTURE_NAME = 'SSI_WORKER_WAIT_INFO'
        IS_LAYOUT        = L_LIST_LAYOUT
      CHANGING
        IT_OUTTAB        = WAITINFO_TAB
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS           = 0 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_WAITINFO.
    DATA: WORKER_WAIT_INFO TYPE SSI_WORKER_WAIT_INFO,
          TIME_T_BIAS      TYPE P VALUE '19700101000000',
          TSTMP            TYPE P,
          TIMESTAMP(64),
          WAIT_INFO(128).

    CLEAR WAITINFO_TAB.

    IF WORKER_INFO-START_OF_WAIT > 0.
      "tstmp = utclong_add( val = time_t_bias  seconds = worker_info-start_of_wait ).
      CL_ABAP_TSTMP=>ADD(
        EXPORTING
          TSTMP   = TIME_T_BIAS " UTC Time Stamp
          SECS    = WORKER_INFO-START_OF_WAIT " Time Interval in Seconds
        RECEIVING
          R_TSTMP = TSTMP " UTC Time Stamp
      ).
      " TODO: what time zone should be used?
      "tzone = sy-zonlo. " seems to be the user time zone
      WRITE TSTMP TIME ZONE SY-ZONLO TO TIMESTAMP.
      WORKER_WAIT_INFO-START_OF_WAIT = TIMESTAMP(19).
    ENDIF.

    WORKER_WAIT_INFO-WAIT_FOR = CL_WORKER_INFO=>MAP_STATE_INFO_2_STR( WORKER_INFO-STATE_INFO ).
    CASE WORKER_INFO-STATE_INFO.
      WHEN WAITING_FOR_RFC.
        WAIT_INFO = TEXT-039.
        REPLACE '&' WITH WORKER_INFO-WAIT_INFO INTO WAIT_INFO.
        REPLACE '&' WITH WORKER_INFO-WAIT_FOR INTO WAIT_INFO.
        CONDENSE WAIT_INFO.
        WORKER_WAIT_INFO-WAIT_INFO = WAIT_INFO.

      WHEN WAITING_FOR_ADM.
        WAIT_INFO = TEXT-038.
        REPLACE '&' WITH WORKER_INFO-WAIT_INFO INTO WAIT_INFO.
        REPLACE '&' WITH WORKER_INFO-WAIT_FOR INTO WAIT_INFO.
        CONDENSE WAIT_INFO.
        WORKER_WAIT_INFO-WAIT_INFO = WAIT_INFO.

      WHEN WAITING_FOR_AMC_ADMIN.
        WAIT_INFO = TEXT-038.
        REPLACE '&' WITH WORKER_INFO-WAIT_INFO INTO WAIT_INFO.
        REPLACE '&' WITH WORKER_INFO-WAIT_FOR INTO WAIT_INFO.
        CONDENSE WAIT_INFO.
        WORKER_WAIT_INFO-WAIT_INFO = WAIT_INFO.

    ENDCASE.

    APPEND WORKER_WAIT_INFO TO WAITINFO_TAB.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_MEMORY_STATISTIC.
    DATA: L_LIST_LAYOUT           TYPE LVC_S_LAYO,
          TITLE(64),
          SELECTED_WORKER_STR(10).

    IF MEMORY_VIEWER IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT MEMORY_VIEWER
        EXPORTING
          I_PARENT = CONTAINER_MEM_INFO.
    ENDIF.

    " Build title
    IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
      TITLE = TEXT-533.
    ELSE.
      WRITE SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX TO SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      TITLE = TEXT-431.
      REPLACE '&' IN TITLE WITH SELECTED_WORKER_STR.

    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_TOOLBAR = NO_TOOLBAR.

    MEMORY_VIEWER->SET_TABLE_FOR_FIRST_DISPLAY(
      EXPORTING

        IS_LAYOUT        = L_LIST_LAYOUT

      EXCEPTIONS

  METHOD GET_MEMORY_STATISTIC.
    DATA: SESSION      TYPE REF TO CL_SESSION_INFO,
          SESSION_INFO TYPE SSI_SESSION_INFO,
          MEM_STAT     TYPE SSI_WORKER_MEM_STATISTIC,
          DIAREC       TYPE PFDIAREC64.

    CLEAR MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.
    CLEAR DIAREC.

    IF WORKER_INFO-LOGON_HDL = CL_SESSION_INFO=>NO_LOGON_HDL OR
       WORKER_INFO-SESSION_HDL = CL_SESSION_INFO=>NO_SESSION_HDL.
      MESSAGE S667.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    TRY.
        CREATE OBJECT SESSION TYPE CL_SESSION_INFO
          EXPORTING
            LOGON_HDL   = WORKER_INFO-LOGON_HDL
            LOGON_ID    = WORKER_INFO-LOGON_ID
            SESSION_HDL = WORKER_INFO-SESSION_HDL.
        SESSION_INFO = SESSION->GET_INFO( WITH_APPLICATION_INFO = 0 ).
      CATCH CX_SSI_INTERNAL CX_SSI_BAD_PARAMETER.
        MESSAGE S667.
        RETURN.
      CATCH CX_SSI_NO_AUTH.
        " Raise status message saying that authorization for
        " memory statistics are missing
        MESSAGE S150 WITH TEXT-533.
        RETURN.
    ENDTRY.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-440.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = SESSION_INFO-MEMORY_BRUTTO.
    APPEND MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-441.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = SESSION_INFO-MEMORY / 1024.
    APPEND  MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-442.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = SESSION_INFO-MEMORY_ABAP / 1024.
    APPEND  MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-443.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = SESSION_INFO-MEMORY_HYPER / 1024.
    APPEND  MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-444.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = SESSION_INFO-MEMORY_HEAP / 1024.
    APPEND  MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-447.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = SESSION_INFO-PAGING_BLOCKS.
    APPEND  MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

    " try to read the statistic of the selected worker
    CALL FUNCTION 'TH_WP_DETAIL_INFO64'
      EXPORTING
        WP_INDEX = SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX
      IMPORTING
        DIAREC64 = DIAREC.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-445.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = DIAREC-MAXBYTES / 1024.
    APPEND MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

    MEM_STAT-TYPE = TEXT-446.
    MEM_STAT-MEM_SIZE = DIAREC-MAXBYTESDI / 1024.
    APPEND MEM_STAT TO MEMORY_STATISTIC_TAB.

  ENDMETHOD.

    ELSE.

      EXPORTING
        I_STRUCTURE_NAME = 'SSI_APPLICATION_INFO_LINE'

        IT_OUTTAB        = APPLICATION_INFO_TAB
      EXCEPTIONS

  METHOD GET_APPLICATION_INFO.
    DATA: LEVEL                 TYPE I,
          APPL_INFO             TYPE THFB_APPL_INFO,
          APPL_INFO_LEN         TYPE I,
          APPLICATION_INFO      TYPE SSI_APPLICATION_INFO_LINE,
          READ_APPLICATION_INFO TYPE I VALUE 1.

    CLEAR APPLICATION_INFO_TAB.

    " try to read the application info of the selected worker
    " First read stack info
    LEVEL = 0.
    WHILE READ_APPLICATION_INFO = 1.
      CALL FUNCTION 'TH_GET_APPL_INFO'
        EXPORTING
          LEVEL         = LEVEL
          MODE          = TH_APPL_INFO_MODE_STACK
          WP_INDEX      = SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX
          TIMEOUT       = 100
        IMPORTING
          APPL_INFO     = APPL_INFO
          APPL_INFO_LEN = APPL_INFO_LEN
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.

      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
        READ_APPLICATION_INFO = 0.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      APPLICATION_INFO-TYPE = TEXT-450.
      APPLICATION_INFO-INFO = APPL_INFO(APPL_INFO_LEN).
      APPEND APPLICATION_INFO TO APPLICATION_INFO_TAB.

      LEVEL = LEVEL + 1.
    ENDWHILE.

    " Second read scroll info
    LEVEL = 0.
    READ_APPLICATION_INFO = 1.
    WHILE READ_APPLICATION_INFO = 1.
      CALL FUNCTION 'TH_GET_APPL_INFO'
        EXPORTING
          LEVEL         = LEVEL
          MODE          = TH_APPL_INFO_MODE_SCROLL
          WP_INDEX      = SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX
          TIMEOUT       = 100
        IMPORTING
          APPL_INFO     = APPL_INFO
          APPL_INFO_LEN = APPL_INFO_LEN
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS        = 1.

      IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
        READ_APPLICATION_INFO = 0.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      APPLICATION_INFO-TYPE = TEXT-451.
      APPLICATION_INFO-INFO = APPL_INFO(APPL_INFO_LEN).
      APPEND APPLICATION_INFO TO APPLICATION_INFO_TAB.

      LEVEL = LEVEL + 1.
    ENDWHILE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_ATTRIBUTES.
    DATA: L_LIST_LAYOUT           TYPE LVC_S_LAYO,
          TITLE(64),
          SELECTED_WORKER_STR(10).

    IF ATTRIBUTE_VIEWER IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT ATTRIBUTE_VIEWER
        EXPORTING
          I_PARENT = CONTAINER_ATTR_INFO.
    ENDIF.

    " Build title
    IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
      TITLE = TEXT-531.
    ELSE.
      WRITE SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX TO SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      TITLE = TEXT-433.
      REPLACE '&' IN TITLE WITH SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      CONDENSE TITLE.
    ENDIF.

    L_LIST_LAYOUT-GRID_TITLE = TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-SMALLTITLE = ' '.    "The list title has small fonts,
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-CWIDTH_OPT = 'X'.    "the column width is adjusted,
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_TOOLBAR = ' '.    "the toolbar is suppressed.

    " Always use set_table_for_first display to get auto fitting column sizes
    ATTRIBUTE_VIEWER->SET_TABLE_FOR_FIRST_DISPLAY(
      EXPORTING
        I_STRUCTURE_NAME = 'SSI_WORKER_ATTRIBUTE_INFO'
        IS_LAYOUT        = L_LIST_LAYOUT
      CHANGING
        IT_OUTTAB        = ATTRIBUTE_TAB
        IT_FIELDCATALOG  = ATTRIBUTE_TAB_FC
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS           = 0 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_ATTRIBUTES.
    DATA: WORKER TYPE REF TO CL_WORKER_INFO.

    CLEAR ATTRIBUTE_TAB.

    " try to read the memory statistic of the selected worker
    TRY.
        CREATE OBJECT WORKER TYPE CL_WORKER_INFO
          EXPORTING
            INDEX = SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX.

        ATTRIBUTE_TAB = WORKER->GET_ATTRIBUTES( ).
      CATCH CX_SSI_NO_AUTH.
        MESSAGE E150 WITH TEXT-540.
        "MESSAGE ID 14 TYPE 'E' NUMBER 150 WITH text-540.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_SQL.
    DATA: L_LIST_LAYOUT           TYPE LVC_S_LAYO,
          TITLE(64),
          SELECTED_WORKER_STR(10).

    IF SQLDISP <> 'X'.
      MESSAGE S672.
    ENDIF.

    IF SQL_VIEWER IS INITIAL.
      CREATE OBJECT SQL_VIEWER
        EXPORTING
          I_PARENT = CONTAINER_SQL.
      "first_display = 1.
    ENDIF.

    " Build title
    IF SINGLE_WP_VIEW = 1.
      TITLE = TEXT-536.
    ELSE.
      WRITE SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX TO SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      TITLE = TEXT-438.
      REPLACE '&' IN TITLE WITH SELECTED_WORKER_STR.
      CONDENSE TITLE.
    ENDIF.

    IF LINES( SQL_TAB ) = 0 AND ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'SINGLE'.
      MESSAGE S671.
    ENDIF.

    L_LIST_LAYOUT-GRID_TITLE = TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-SMALLTITLE = SMALL_TITLE.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-CWIDTH_OPT = 'X'.
    L_LIST_LAYOUT-NO_TOOLBAR = NO_TOOLBAR.

    " Calculate source line from offset.
    CALC_SQL_LINE( ).

    " Always use set_table_for_first display to get auto fitting column sizes
    SQL_VIEWER->SET_TABLE_FOR_FIRST_DISPLAY(
      EXPORTING
        I_STRUCTURE_NAME = 'SSI_SQL_INFO_DISP'
        IS_LAYOUT        = L_LIST_LAYOUT
      CHANGING
        IT_OUTTAB        = SQL_TAB_DISP
        IT_FIELDCATALOG  = SQL_TAB_FC
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS           = 0 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD GET_SQL.
    DATA: SQL_TEXT(4096),
          SQL_LEN        TYPE I,
          OFFSET         TYPE I,
          MOVE_PROGRAM   TYPE I,
          SOURCE_REF     TYPE PROG_REF_T,
          SQL_LINE       TYPE SSI_SQL_INFO,
          PID            TYPE WPPID.

    CLEAR SQL_TAB.

    IF SQLDISP <> 'X'.
      " MESSAGE s672. Nasty
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    IF WORKER_INFO-STATE <> 4 AND " not running
       WORKER_INFO-STATE <> 8.    " not holding.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    " try to read the SQL info of the selected worker

    " cast to old type used by DB_WP_CURRENT_SQL.
    PID = WORKER_INFO-PID.
    CALL FUNCTION 'DB_WP_CURRENT_SQL'
      EXPORTING
        WP_PID        = PID
      IMPORTING
        SQL_TEXT      = SQL_TEXT
        SQL_LEN       = SQL_LEN
        SOURCE_REF    = SOURCE_REF
      EXCEPTIONS
        SQL_NOT_FOUND = 1
        OTHERS        = 99.

    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    " Split SQL statement string into lines of sql_statement_width characters
    OFFSET = 0.
    MOVE_PROGRAM = 1.
    WHILE SQL_LEN > SQL_STATEMENT_WIDTH.
      CLEAR SQL_LINE.
      SQL_LINE-STATEMENT = SQL_TEXT+OFFSET(SQL_STATEMENT_WIDTH).
      OFFSET = OFFSET + SQL_STATEMENT_WIDTH.
      SQL_LEN = SQL_LEN - SQL_STATEMENT_WIDTH.
      IF MOVE_PROGRAM = 1.
        SQL_LINE-PROGRAM = SOURCE_REF-PROG_NAME.
        SQL_LINE-OFFSET = SOURCE_REF-CONT_OFFS.
        MOVE_PROGRAM = 0.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND SQL_LINE TO SQL_TAB.
    ENDWHILE.
    IF SQL_LEN > 0.
      CLEAR SQL_LINE.
      SQL_LINE-STATEMENT = SQL_TEXT+OFFSET(SQL_LEN).
      IF MOVE_PROGRAM = 1.
        SQL_LINE-PROGRAM = SOURCE_REF-PROG_NAME.
        SQL_LINE-OFFSET = SOURCE_REF-CONT_OFFS.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND SQL_LINE TO SQL_TAB.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD INITIALIZE_SQL_TAB_FC.
    DATA: FC_LINE     TYPE LVC_S_FCAT,
          COL_POS     TYPE I VALUE 0,
          TABNAME(40).

    CLEAR SQL_TAB_FC.
    TABNAME = 'SQL_TAB'.

    CLEAR FC_LINE.
    COL_POS = COL_POS + 1.
    FC_LINE-FIELDNAME = 'STATEMENT'.
    FC_LINE-TABNAME   = TABNAME.
    FC_LINE-DATATYPE  = 'SSI_SQL_STATEMENT'.
    FC_LINE-JUST      = 'L'.
    FC_LINE-OUTPUTLEN = SQL_STATEMENT_WIDTH.
    FC_LINE-COL_POS   = COL_POS.
    APPEND FC_LINE TO SQL_TAB_FC.

    CLEAR FC_LINE.
    COL_POS = COL_POS + 1.
    FC_LINE-FIELDNAME = 'PROGRAM'.
    FC_LINE-TABNAME   = TABNAME.
    FC_LINE-DATATYPE  = 'SSI_PROGRAM'.
    FC_LINE-JUST      = 'L'.
    FC_LINE-OUTPUTLEN = 32.
    FC_LINE-COL_POS   = COL_POS.
    FC_LINE-NO_OUT    = ' '.
    APPEND FC_LINE TO SQL_TAB_FC.

    CLEAR FC_LINE.
    COL_POS = COL_POS + 1.
    FC_LINE-FIELDNAME = 'LINE_DISP'.
    FC_LINE-TABNAME   = TABNAME.
    FC_LINE-DATATYPE  = 'SSI_SQL_LINE_DISP'.
    FC_LINE-JUST      = 'L'.
    FC_LINE-OUTPUTLEN = 10.
    FC_LINE-COL_POS   = COL_POS.
    FC_LINE-NO_OUT    = ' '.
    APPEND FC_LINE TO SQL_TAB_FC.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CALC_SQL_LINE.
    DATA: LINE        TYPE SSI_SQL_INFO,
          LINE_DISP   TYPE SSI_SQL_INFO_DISP,
          L_RLREF_TAB TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF RLREF,
          OFFSET      TYPE I.

    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
      <L_RLREF>   LIKE LINE OF L_RLREF_TAB.

    CLEAR SQL_TAB_DISP.

    " Here comes magic from function modul DB_SQL_SOURCE_DISPLAY to
    " calculate the correct source line of the sql statement
    LOOP AT SQL_TAB INTO LINE.
      CLEAR LINE_DISP.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING LINE TO LINE_DISP.
      IF LINE-OFFSET > 0.
        OFFSET = LINE-OFFSET.
        IF LINE-PROGRAM IS INITIAL.
          RETURN.
        ENDIF.

        LOAD REPORT LINE-PROGRAM PART 'LREF' INTO L_RLREF_TAB.
        IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
          RETURN.
        ENDIF.

        ##NEEDED
        LOOP AT L_RLREF_TAB ASSIGNING <L_RLREF>
          WHERE OFFSET <= OFFSET.  "int. Problem 2129176 2002
        ENDLOOP.
        IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
          RETURN.
        ENDIF.

        LINE_DISP-LINE_DISP = <L_RLREF>-LINE.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND LINE_DISP TO SQL_TAB_DISP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CLEAR_DETAIL_CONTAINER.

    CLEAR STACK_VIEWER.
    CLEAR WAITINFO_VIEWER.
    CLEAR STATISTIC_VIEWER.
    CLEAR MEMORY_VIEWER.
    CLEAR APPLICATION_INFO_VIEWER.
    CLEAR ATTRIBUTE_VIEWER.
    CLEAR SQL_VIEWER.

  ENDMETHOD.

  " refresh the worker list
  "
  " behavior depends on the selection mode:
  " - if we have to keep the selection:
  "   --> refresh on detail screen
  "   --> get new worker list
  " - if we have to set a new selection
  "   --> double klick on detail screen or detail button pressed
  "   --> keep the worker list
  METHOD REFRESH_WORKER_LIST.
    DATA: NEW_ROW_SELECTION TYPE I VALUE -1,
          OLD_ROW_SELECTION TYPE I VALUE -1.

    CASE WORKER_SELECTION.
      WHEN KEEP_SELECTION.
        NEW_ROW_SELECTION = -1.

      WHEN SET_NEW_SELECTION.
        " Get current selection of old worker list
        NEW_ROW_SELECTION = GET_WORKER_SELECTION_FROM_LIST( ).
        INVALIDATE_WORKER_SELECTION( ).
    ENDCASE.

    " Get new worker list
    IF WORKER_SELECTION <> SET_NEW_SELECTION.
      GET_WORKER_LIST( ).
      SHOW_WORKER_LIST( EXPORTING FIRST_DISPLAY = 0 ).
    ENDIF.

    IF WORKER_SELECTION = KEEP_SELECTION.
      " If there is still a valid prevous selection: search selected wp in new worker list
      OLD_ROW_SELECTION = GET_SELECTED_WORKER_FROM_INDEX( INDEX = SELECTED_WORKER_INDEX ).
      IF OLD_ROW_SELECTION <> -1.
        NEW_ROW_SELECTION = OLD_ROW_SELECTION.
      ELSE.
        OLD_ROW_SELECTION = -2.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    " If there is a new selection: keep selected index and mark selected wp in list
    IF NEW_ROW_SELECTION <> -1.
      SET_WORKER_SELECTION_IN_LIST( EXPORTING ROW = NEW_ROW_SELECTION ).
    ELSE.
      "invalidate_worker_selection( ).
      CLEAR_WORKER_SELECTION_IN_LIST( ).
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD REFRESH_DETAIL_CONTAINER.
    IF ACTIVE_DETAIL_VIEW = 'SINGLE'.
      CASE ACTIVE_DETAIL_CONTROL.
        WHEN 'RUNTIME_INFO'.
          " IF withrinf = ' '.
          "   MESSAGE s673. Don't display nasty message
          " ENDIF.
          SHOW_RUNTIME_INFO( ).
          RETURN.

        WHEN 'DB'.
          SHOW_DB_STATISTIC( ).
          RETURN.

        WHEN 'MEMORY'.
          SHOW_MEMORY_STATISTIC( ).
          RETURN.

        WHEN 'APPLICATION'.
          SHOW_APPLICATION_INFO( ).
          RETURN.

        WHEN 'ATTRIBUTES'.
          SHOW_ATTRIBUTES( ).
          RETURN.

        WHEN 'SQL'.
          SHOW_SQL( ).
          RETURN.

      ENDCASE.
    ELSE.
      SHOW_COMPACT_INFO( ).
      RETURN.

    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD SHOW_COMPACT_INFO.
    CREATE_COMPACT_VIEW( ).
    SHOW_RUNTIME_INFO( ).
    SHOW_DB_STATISTIC( ).
    SHOW_MEMORY_STATISTIC( ).
    IF SQLDISP = 'X'.
      SHOW_SQL( ).
    ELSE.
      SHOW_APPLICATION_INFO( ).
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CREATE_SINGLE_VIEW.
    IF SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE IS NOT INITIAL.
      CONTAINER_RUNTIME = SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 1 ).
      CONTAINER_MEM_INFO = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
      CONTAINER_STATISTIC = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
      CONTAINER_APPL_INFO = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
      CONTAINER_ATTR_INFO = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
      CONTAINER_SQL = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    CREATE OBJECT SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE
      EXPORTING
        PARENT  = CONTAINER_DETAIL_INFO
        ROWS    = 1
        COLUMNS = 1.

    " Set heigh of upper screen
    SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE->SET_ROW_HEIGHT(
      EXPORTING
        ID     = 1
        HEIGHT = 40 ).

    CONTAINER_RUNTIME = SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 1 ).
    CONTAINER_MEM_INFO = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    CONTAINER_STATISTIC = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    CONTAINER_APPL_INFO = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    CONTAINER_ATTR_INFO = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    CONTAINER_SQL = CONTAINER_RUNTIME.

    "small_title = ' '.
    NO_TOOLBAR = ' '.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD DELETE_SINGLE_VIEW.
    IF SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE IS NOT INITIAL.
      SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE->FREE( ).
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR SPLITTER_DETAIL_SINGLE.

    CLEAR CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_MEM_INFO.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_STATISTIC.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_APPL_INFO.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_ATTR_INFO.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_SQL.

    CLEAR_DETAIL_CONTAINER( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD CREATE_COMPACT_VIEW.
    DATA: LV_CONTAINER_RUNTIME TYPE REF TO CL_GUI_CONTAINER.

    IF SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT IS NOT INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    CREATE OBJECT SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT
      EXPORTING
        PARENT  = CONTAINER_DETAIL_INFO
        ROWS    = 2
        COLUMNS = 1.

    " Set heigh of upper screen
    SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT->SET_ROW_HEIGHT(
      EXPORTING
        ID     = 1
        HEIGHT = 40 ).

    CONTAINER_INFO_TOP = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 1 ).
    LV_CONTAINER_RUNTIME = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 2 COLUMN = 1 ).

    CREATE OBJECT SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP
      EXPORTING
        PARENT  = CONTAINER_INFO_TOP
        ROWS    = 1
        COLUMNS = 3.

    " Set column witdth for memory and statistic column
    SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP->SET_COLUMN_WIDTH(
      EXPORTING
        ID    = 1
        WIDTH = 20 ).
    SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP->SET_COLUMN_WIDTH(
      EXPORTING
        ID    = 2
        WIDTH = 30 ).

    CREATE OBJECT SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM
      EXPORTING
        PARENT  = LV_CONTAINER_RUNTIME
        ROWS    = 1
        COLUMNS = 1.

    SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM->SET_COLUMN_WIDTH(
      EXPORTING
        ID    = 1
        WIDTH = 28 ).

    CONTAINER_RUNTIME = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 1 ).

    CONTAINER_MEM_INFO = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 1 ).
    CONTAINER_STATISTIC = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 2 ).
    IF SQLDISP = 'X'.
      CONTAINER_SQL = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 3 ).
    ELSE.
      CONTAINER_APPL_INFO = SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP->GET_CONTAINER( ROW = 1 COLUMN = 3 ).
    ENDIF.

    " Configure layout: no toolbar, small title font
    "small_title = 'X'.
    NO_TOOLBAR = 'X'.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD DELETE_COMPACT_VIEW.

    IF SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP IS NOT INITIAL.
      SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP->FREE( ).
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_TOP.
    IF SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM IS NOT INITIAL.
      SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM->FREE( ).
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT_BOTTOM.

    IF SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT IS NOT INITIAL.
      SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT->FREE( ).
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR SPLITTER_DETAIL_COMPACT.

    CLEAR CONTAINER_RUNTIME.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_MEM_INFO.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_STATISTIC.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_SQL.
    CLEAR CONTAINER_APPL_INFO.

    CLEAR_DETAIL_CONTAINER( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

ENDCLASS.                    "detail_info_screen_handler IMPLEMENTATION

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*& Processing Blocks called by the Runtime Environment                 *          &nb
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb

* Dialog Module PBO                                     
MODULE STATUS_2000 OUTPUT.
  ##DECL_MODUL
  DATA: CALLED_FROM_MAIN_LIST TYPE I.

  SET PF-STATUS 'SCREEN_2000' EXCLUDING EXTAB_FOR_DETAIL.
  SET TITLEBAR '400'.

  " Resizing the window calls this with initial okcode
  IF DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>GET_CONTINUE_SCREEN( ) = 0.
    CALLED_FROM_MAIN_LIST = 1.
  ELSE.
    CALLED_FROM_MAIN_LIST = 0.
  ENDIF.

  IF CALLED_FROM_MAIN_LIST = 1.
    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>CREATE_SCREEN(
      WORKER_SELECTION = DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SET_NEW_SELECTION
      WORKER_INDEX     = SEL_WP_INDEX ).
  ELSE.
    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>CONTINUE_SCREEN( ).
  ENDIF.

  "sel_wp_index = -1.

ENDMODULE.                    "status_2000 OUTPUT

  LEAVE PROGRAM.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*&      Module  2000_AFTER_INPUT  INPUT                           &nbs
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*                                          &n
* This module is called                                   &n
* - for all okcodes on main menue: back, exit, cancel                         &n
* - for okcode DETAIL on Detail screen                              &n
* - for okcode REFR_DET on Detail screen                              
* - some other okcodes during resizing the window                           &nbs
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
MODULE 2000_AFTER_INPUT INPUT.                              "#EC WARNOK
  ##DECL_MODUL
  DATA: WORKER_SELECTION TYPE I VALUE DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>KEEP_SELECTION.

  UCOMM = OKCODE.

  IF UCOMM = 'BACK' OR
     UCOMM = 'EXIT' OR
     UCOMM = 'CANCEL'.
    CLEAR OKCODE.
    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SCREEN->TOGGLE_TO_COMPACT_VIEW( ).
    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SCREEN->SET_CONT_SCREEN( 0 ).
    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SCREEN->INVALIDATE_WORKER_SELECTION( ).
    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SCREEN->CLEAR_TABLES( ).
    SEL_WP_INDEX = -1.
    SET SCREEN 0.
  ELSE.
    IF UCOMM = 'DETAIL'.
      WORKER_SELECTION = DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SET_NEW_SELECTION.
    ENDIF.

    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SCREEN->SET_CONT_SCREEN( 1 ).
    DETAIL_INFO_SCREEN_HANDLER=>SCREEN->REFRESH_ALL(
      WORKER_SELECTION = WORKER_SELECTION ).
  ENDIF.
  CLEAR OKCODE.
ENDMODULE.                 " 2000_AFTER_INPUT  INPUT

MODULE STATUS_3000 OUTPUT.
  SET PF-STATUS 'DETAIL'.
  SET TITLEBAR '101'.

  IF FIRST_DETAIL_DISPLAY = 'X'.
    FIRST_DETAIL_DISPLAY = ' '.

    CREATE OBJECT CONTAINER
      EXPORTING
        REPID     = SY-REPID
        DYNNR     = '3000'
        SIDE      = CL_GUI_DOCKING_CONTAINER=>DOCK_AT_LEFT    " Side to which this control is docked to
        EXTENSION = 2500.

    CREATE OBJECT DOCUMENT.
    DOCUMENT->INITIALIZE_DOCUMENT( BACKGROUND_COLOR = CL_DD_AREA=>COL_TREE_LEVEL1 ).

    PERFORM ADD_CONTENT.
    PERFORM DISPLAY_DETAIL.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM REFRESH_DOCUMENT.
  ENDIF.
ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_3000  OUTPUT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*&      Module  USER_COMMAND_3000  INPUT                           &nb
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*       text                                  &nbs
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
MODULE USER_COMMAND_3000 INPUT.
  CASE SY-UCOMM(4).
    WHEN 'BACK'.
      LEAVE SCREEN.
    WHEN 'EXIT'.
      LEAVE PROGRAM.
    WHEN '%_GC'.
      CALL METHOD CL_GUI_CFW=>DISPATCH.
      LEAVE TO SCREEN 3000.
    WHEN 'REFR'.
      LEAVE TO SCREEN 3000.
  ENDCASE.
ENDMODULE.                 " USER_COMMAND_3000  INPUT

        TEXT = <ABAP_STACK_LINE>-EVENTTYPE.
        GRID_COL_5->ADD_TEXT( TEXT = TEXT ).
        TEXT = <ABAP_STACK_LINE>-EVENTNAME.
        GRID_COL_6->ADD_TEXT( TEXT = TEXT ).
        TEXT = <ABAP_STACK_LINE>-CLASS.

        TEXT = <ABAP_STACK_LINE>-FLAG_SYSTEM.
        GRID_COL_8->ADD_TEXT( TEXT = TEXT ).
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM ADD_EMPTY_LINE.

    " ELSE.
    "  MESSAGE s673. Don't display nasty message
  ENDIF. " ABAP stack info

ENDFORM.

FORM ADD_EMPTY_LINE_INTERNAL USING LOOPS TYPE I.
  DATA: GRID_TABLE TYPE REF TO CL_DD_TABLE_ELEMENT,
        GRID_COL   TYPE REF TO CL_DD_AREA.

  DOCUMENT->ADD_TABLE(
    EXPORTING
      NO_OF_COLUMNS = 1
      BORDER        = '0'    " Breite des Tabellenrahmens; wenn '0', kein Rahmen
      WIDTH         = '100%'    " Breite der Tabelle; wenn '100%', ganze Controlbreite
      WITH_HEADING  = ' '
    IMPORTING
      TABLE         = GRID_TABLE
  ).

  ##NO_TEXT
  GRID_TABLE->ADD_COLUMN(
    EXPORTING
      WIDTH   = '100%'    " Breite der Spalte, z.B. '20%'
      HEADING = 'Spalte21' ##NO_TEXT
    IMPORTING
      COLUMN  = GRID_COL
  ).
  DO LOOPS TIMES.
    GRID_TABLE->NEW_ROW( ).
    GRID_COL->ADD_TEXT( TEXT = '' ).
  ENDDO.

ENDFORM.

FORM ADD_SHORT_EMPTY_LINE.
  PERFORM ADD_EMPTY_LINE_INTERNAL USING 5.
ENDFORM.

FORM DISPLAY_DETAIL.
  DOCUMENT->MERGE_DOCUMENT( ).

  DOCUMENT->DISPLAY_DOCUMENT(
    EXPORTING
      PARENT             = CONTAINER
      REUSE_CONTROL      = 'X'
      REUSE_REGISTRATION = 'X'
  ).

ENDFORM. " display_detail

FORM REFRESH_DOCUMENT.
  DOCUMENT->INITIALIZE_DOCUMENT( BACKGROUND_COLOR = CL_DD_AREA=>COL_TREE_LEVEL1 ).
  PERFORM ADD_CONTENT.
  PERFORM DISPLAY_DETAIL.
ENDFORM.

*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       FORM wp_db_agent_display                              &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
*       display details of assigned DB agent                         &
*       D037596 31.01.2011                               &nb
*---------------------------------------------------------------------                               
FORM WP_DB_AGENT_DISPLAY.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
    <WPINFO>           LIKE LINE OF WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV.
  DATA:
    WP_PID TYPE WPPID,
    SEL_IX TYPE I.

  LOOP AT WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV ASSIGNING <WPINFO>.
    IF <WPINFO>-SELECTED = ABAP_TRUE.
      SEL_IX = SY-TABIX.
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF <WPINFO>-SELECTED <> ABAP_TRUE.
    MESSAGE S101.
  ELSE.
    READ TABLE WORKER_LIST_DISP_ALV INDEX SEL_IX ASSIGNING <WPINFO>.
    WP_PID = <WPINFO>-PID.
    CALL FUNCTION 'DBA_WP_DB_AGENT_DETAILS'
      EXPORTING
        WP_PID      = WP_PID
        WP_HOSTNAME = SY-HOST
      EXCEPTIONS
        OTHERS      = 1.
    IF SY-SUBRC <> 0.
      MESSAGE E087.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM. "wp_db_agent_display
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*& Form AUTO_REFRESH                                     &nb
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*& text                                        &nb
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*& -->  p1        text                             &
*& <--  p2        text                             &
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
FORM AUTO_REFRESH .

  DATA : LV_INTERVAL   TYPE I.
  DATA : LT_FIELDS     TYPE TABLE OF SVAL WITH HEADER LINE,
         LV_RETURNCODE.

  CLEAR LV_RETURNCODE.
  LT_FIELDS-TABNAME   = 'SYST'.
  LT_FIELDS-FIELDNAME = 'INDEX'.
  LT_FIELDS-VALUE     = '60'.
  LT_FIELDS-FIELDTEXT = '자동 REFRESH(초)'.
  APPEND LT_FIELDS.

  CALL FUNCTION 'POPUP_GET_VALUES'
    EXPORTING
      NO_VALUE_CHECK  = ' '
      POPUP_TITLE     = '자동 Refresh'
      START_COLUMN    = '5'
      START_ROW       = '5'
    IMPORTING
      RETURNCODE      = LV_RETURNCODE
    TABLES
      FIELDS          = LT_FIELDS
    EXCEPTIONS
      ERROR_IN_FIELDS = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.

  ""취소버튼
  IF LV_RETURNCODE EQ 'A'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  READ TABLE LT_FIELDS INDEX 1.
  IF LT_FIELDS-VALUE IS INITIAL.
    EXIT.

  ELSE.
    LV_INTERVAL = LT_FIELDS-VALUE.
  ENDIF.

  IF G_TIMER IS INITIAL.
    CREATE OBJECT G_TIMER.
    CREATE OBJECT G_TIMER_RCVER.
  ENDIF.

  SET HANDLER G_TIMER_RCVER->HANDLE_FINISHED FOR G_TIMER.

*  LV_INTERVAL = 60.                                    
  G_TIMER->INTERVAL = LV_INTERVAL.

  CALL METHOD G_TIMER->RUN.

ENDFORM.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
*       CLASS LCL_TIMER_RCVER IMPLEMENTATION                           &nbs
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*                              &nb
CLASS LCL_TIMER_RCVER IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD HANDLE_FINISHED.

*    PERFORM START.                                   &
    PERFORM BUILD_LOCAL_LIST.
*    RS_SELFIELD-REFRESH = 'X'.                                &n
*    RS_SELFIELD-COL_STABLE = 'X'.                                
*    RS_SELFIELD-ROW_STABLE = 'X'.                                

    CALL METHOD G_TIMER->RUN.
    CALL METHOD CL_GUI_CFW=>SET_NEW_OK_CODE
      EXPORTING
        NEW_CODE = 'ONLI'.
  ENDMETHOD.                    "HANDLE_FINISHED
ENDCLASS. "LCL_RECEIVER IMPLEMENTATION
